# Intake specs: motion-reference.takayustudio.jp, what to build

Part of the intake [2026-10-12-motion-reference-takayustudio.md](2026-10-12-motion-reference-takayustudio.md); the full list of 782
motions is in [2026-10-12-motion-reference-takayustudio-catalog.md](2026-10-12-motion-reference-takayustudio-catalog.md).

Read on 2026-10-12. Each id links to the site's own demo (`https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=<id>`). Names, timings
and eases are the site's; status, kit match, target, priority and spec are this intake's judgement against the kit at commit `66161ef`.
**Specs are numbers in our own words, never the site's code.**

Status: **have** = a kit preset or layout does it · **generic** = only the general-purpose `motion/anime` / `motion/tween` do it (no
named preset) · **partial** = the kit has the mechanism but lacks a defining part · **missing** = nothing in the kit · **skip** = a lesson
or an audio-only item with no film motion (imported anyway as a lesson demo: every motion is imported, the user decides). Engine: `canvas` = the site draws a schematic on a 320 x 200 canvas (port the moving subject,
not the guides or readouts) · `dom` = keyframe tracks on elements (320 x 180 stage) · `text` = the site's text engine. Times in ms.

## Build targets (partial and missing), by priority

One target = one kit preset to write (NEW) or an existing preset to extend (EXTEND). The specs under it are the motions it must cover.
Every spec below was checked against the site's data on 2026-10-12 ("verified" or "corrected"); the import manifest
(`2026-10-12-motion-reference-takayustudio-manifest.json`) holds the same specs for all 782 motions, including the ones the kit
already has, with the import name and batch.

### text/land-in: EXTEND · high · effect · ae keyframes · 13 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: codrops (5), other:booth.pm (1).

- [`catalog-game-reward-new-record`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-reward-new-record) **New Record Stamp** (Rewards & Loot; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `data/count-up`, `text/land-in`. Proposal: text/land-in + style stamp.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/new-record-stamp`, batch 3.5): px @320x200. Score 0 → 9,150 outExpo (0.3-1.1 s). Stamp lands at 1.4 s: scale 2.4 → 1 in 140 ms in-cubic (fade in 60 ms), tilted −12°, outlined (2.5 px accent, r 5); on contact squash 12 % (scaleX 1.12 / Y 0.88, 160 ms half-sine); the score number shakes ±2.5 px for 120 ms; 8 seeded ink dots spread from an ellipse 34 x 18 to 50 x 28 px around the stamp over 400 ms outCubic, fading. 'BEST' swaps 8,420 → 9,150 (accent) 50 ms after contact.
- [`catalog-game-impact-fx-mission-stamp`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-impact-fx-mission-stamp) **Mission Stamp Punch** (Game Impact FX; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `text/land-in`, `hand/hit-stop`. Proposal: text/land-in + style stamp.
  Spec (verified; import as `text/stamp-punch`, batch 3.1): px @320x200. Stamp 96 x 38 (3 px outline + 1 px inner outline inset 6 px, accent) drops scale 2.6 → 1 in 220 ms in-quad (0.6-0.82 s), fade in 100 ms, tilted −12.6°; a 35 % dark shadow offset (2, 4) + (scale − 1) × (10, 14) px while above the page. At contact the shrink stops: wobble 1 − 0.06 e^(−18t) cos(40t); the page shakes ±3 px 140 ms; 7 seeded ink dots (1-3 px) splash to 46-64 px (vertical × 0.6) in 120 ms outCubic and stay. Fade 3.6-3.9 s.
- [`tx-directional-blur-slide`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-directional-blur-slide) **Directional Blur Slide** (Blur & Focus; text; 550 ms; easeOutExpo; partial, high). Closest: `effect/motion-blur`, `text/shutter-slam`. Proposal: text/land-in + style whip (slide with horizontal-only blur proportional to speed).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/directional-blur-slide`, batch 3.4): x −200 → 0 px in 0.5 s expo.out (cubic-bezier(.16,1,.3,1)); horizontal-only blur (SVG feGaussianBlur stdDeviation "20 0" → "0 0", ≈40 px spread) on the same spline as the move, so it is 0 exactly when the text stops. CSS fallback: scaleX 1.6 → 1.05 → 1 (origin right) with blur 4 px → 0 and opacity 0 → 1 by 60 %. Canvas demo: −220 px over 0.55 s as a 12-sample trail covering 1/48 s (180° shutter at 24 fps), exit +220 px 2.0–2.4 s expo.in; loop 2.6 s.
- [`tx-drop-bounce-in`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-drop-bounce-in) **Drop & Bounce In** (Glyph Physics; text; 900 ms; bounce.out; partial, high). Closest: `text/land-in`, `text/top-down-letters`. Proposal: text/land-in + param split: chars/words (stagger, from first/last/random).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/drop-bounce`, batch 3.3): Per char: y −200 px → 0, bounce.out 0.9 s, stagger 0.04 s (capped so all start within 1.2 s), from 0.3 s; 3 shrinking rebounds. Optional floor shadow under each char: ellipse 0.9 × char width, scaleY 0.18, scaleX max(0.2, 1 − h / 200), opacity 0.28 × (1 − h / 160), h = height above the floor. Exit 3.15–3.45 s: opacity 1 → 0 and y +30 px, power2.in. First char accent. Loop 3.6 s.
- [`tx-pendulum-swing`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-pendulum-swing) **Pendulum Swing** (Glyph Physics; text; 2400 ms; spring(90, 4.5); partial, medium). Closest: `motion/pendulum-swing`, `motion/inertial-bounce`. Proposal: text/land-in + param split: chars/words (stagger, from first/last/random) + style swing.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/pendulum-letters`, batch 3.3): Per char, pivot at its top centre (50 % 0 %), letter spacing 0.08 em: rotation 90° → 0 by spring k 90, c 4.5, m 1 (ζ≈0.24, ω 9.2 rad/s) from 0.35 s, stagger 0.06 s (capped at 0.9 s total). Peaks: −42° at 0.34 s, +19° at 0.68 s, −9° at 1.02 s, +4° at 1.36 s, settled ~2 s. Exit 3.55–4.15 s: swing back up to 90° with back.in. Optional rail line above the letters with a thin thread and a pivot dot per letter. Loop 4.2 s.
- [`tx-squash-landing`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-squash-landing) **Squash Landing** (Glyph Physics; text; 1100 ms; power2 + elastic.out; partial, medium). Closest: `text/land-in`, `house/hop`. Proposal: text/land-in + param split: chars/words (stagger, from first/last/random) + style hop (stretch / squash).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/squash-hop`, batch 3.3): Per char, origin at the baseline centre, from 0.35 s, stagger 0.06 s (capped at 1 s total). 0–80 ms crouch: sx 1 + 0.08·sin, sy 1 − 0.1·sin (peak 1.08 / 0.9). 80–340 ms rise to −0.6 em power1.out, stretch sx 0.85 → 1, sy 1.2 → 1. 340–540 ms fall power1.in, stretch back to 0.85 / 1.2. 540–620 ms squash to sx 1.25, sy 0.7 power1.out. 620–1120 ms recover to 1 × 1, elastic.out(1, 0.3). Floor shadow widens as the char falls. Loop 2.9 s.
- [`tx-flip-up-3d`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-flip-up-3d) **3D Flip Up** (3D & Depth; text; 800 ms; expo.out; partial, high · origin codrops). Closest: `text/land-in`. Proposal: text/land-in + param split: chars/words (stagger, from first/last/random).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/flip-up-3d`, batch 3.3): Per char: rotateX −90° → 0 about its baseline (origin 50 % 100 %), y +25 % of the glyph box → 0, opacity 0 → 1 reached at 40 % of the move; 0.8 s expo.out (cubic-bezier .16,1,.3,1), stagger 40 ms (capped at 1 s total), from 0.3 s. Perspective about 9 em (demo 460 px at 52 px text; recipe 600–2000 px). Exit 2.5–2.85 s fade power2.in. Loop 3 s.
- [`tx-swing-door`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-swing-door) **Swing Door** (3D & Depth; text; 1000 ms; expo.out; partial, medium · origin codrops). Closest: `text/land-in`. Proposal: text/land-in + param split: chars/words (stagger, from first/last/random) + z offset.
  Spec (verified; import as `text/swing-door`, batch 3.3): Per char: rotateY −90° → 0 about a vertical hinge at its right edge (90 % of the glyph width), z −300 → 0 with perspective 2000 px (demo: z −0.15 × perspective), 1 s expo.out (cubic-bezier .16,1,.3,1), stagger 0.06 s from the last letter (capped at 1.1 s total), from 0.3 s. Exit 2.7–3.0 s fade power2.in. Loop 3.2 s.
- [`tx-z-fly-in`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-z-fly-in) **Fly In From Depth** (3D & Depth; text; 900 ms; back.out(1.2); partial, medium · origin codrops). Closest: `text/land-in`, `text/shared-axis-z`. Proposal: text/land-in + param split: chars/words (stagger, from first/last/random) + style zfly.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/depth-fly-in`, batch 3.3): Per char: z −800 → 0 with perspective 1000 px toward the frame centre (position and scale × P / (P − z)), back.out(1.2) 0.9 s (slight overshoot past z 0), stagger 0.04 s (capped at 0.9 s total), from 0.3 s; opacity = 0.2 → 1 following z. Three accent trail copies 30 / 60 / 90 ms behind at opacity 0.24 / 0.18 / 0.12. Exit 2.4–2.75 s fade power2.in. Loop 3.2 s.
- [`tx-tumble-in`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-tumble-in) **Tumbling 3D Entrance** (3D & Depth; text; 1300 ms; power1.inOut; partial, medium · origin codrops). Closest: `text/land-in`. Proposal: text/land-in + param split: chars/words (stagger, from first/last/random) + style tumble (seeded per-char ranges).
  Spec (verified; import as `text/tumble-in-3d`, batch 3.3): Per char, seeded: z from −1500…−600 → 0 and rotateX from −500…−200° → 0 about the glyph's horizontal centre line, perspective 2000 px; 1.3 s power1.inOut, stagger 0.05 s (capped at 0.8 s total), from 0.3 s; opacity reaches 1 at 33 % of the move; back faces shaded darker (×0.45). Exit 2.8–3.15 s fade power2.in. Loop 3.4 s.
- [`tx-horizontal-letter-drop`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-horizontal-letter-drop) **Horizontal Scroll Letter Drop** (Scroll; text; 4000 ms; back.out(2) (scrub); partial, medium · origin codrops). Closest: `text/land-in`, `text/top-down-letters`. Proposal: text/land-in + param split: chars/words (stagger, from first/last/random) + style dropScale.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/letter-drop-slide`, batch 3.2): Progress p 0 → 1 over 0.3–2.3 s, cubic-bezier(.3,0,.5,1), back 3.1–3.7 s. Title shifts in from the right by its width + 40 px, linear in p, done at p = 0.7. Letter i: q = (p − 0.12 − i × each) / 0.45, each = min(0.05, 0.4 / (n − 1)) of p; b = back.out(2)(q): y −2.9 em × (1 − b) (demo 180 px at 62 px; recipe yPercent −900), scale 3 → 1 by b (min 0.2), origin baseline; opacity reaches 1 at q = 0.25. Loop 4 s.
- [`tx-lyric-tame-tsume`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-lyric-tame-tsume) **Lyric Motion Tame/Tsume** (Japanese; text; 300 ms; cubic-bezier(.9,0,.1,1); partial, medium · origin other:booth.pm). Closest: `text/land-in`, `text/lyric-line`, `hand/boil`. Proposal: text/land-in + param fps (posterize, 12) + ease alias tametsume in eases.js.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/tame-tsume`, batch 3.5): Time posterized to 12 fps (83 ms steps). Units = words (chars if fewer than 3 words); beats every 0.32 s (capped at 1.9 s total) from 0.55 s. Each unit moves in during the 0.3 s before its beat with cubic-bezier(.9,0,.1,1): from x −70 px / rot −20°, then y −46 px / +14°, x +70 px / +20°, y +46 px / −14° (cycling), scale 1.25 → 1, to a seeded rest offset (x ±2 px, y ±5 px, ±3°). Accent for 2 frames (167 ms) on its beat. All cut at 3.25 s. Loop 3.6 s.
- [`tx-stamp-cutout`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-stamp-cutout) **Rubber-Stamp Cut-out Pop** (Titles & Film; text; 167 ms; steps(2); partial, medium). Closest: `text/land-in`, `text/texture-mask`, `card/poster-tile-mosaic`. Proposal: text/land-in + style stamp (12 fps overshoot).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/stamp-pop`, batch 3.2): Posterized 12 fps. Units = words (chars if one word); beats every min(0.36, 1.7 / n) s from 0.4 s. On its beat a unit appears at scale 1.18, next 12 fps frame 0.95, then 1.0 (no tweening), with a seeded tilt ±3.4°. The background card hard-switches each beat: accent → second accent → yellow (text in background colour). Flat shapes (a 16 px dot, a small rectangle) stamp on beats 1 and 2. Ink voids: ~90 holes 0.5–1.8 px punched out of the letters. All off at 3.42 s. Loop 3.8 s.

### motion/camera: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 12 motion(s)

- [`cam-pushin`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=cam-pushin) **Push In / Dolly In** (Camera; dom; 1600 ms; easeInOutCubic; partial, high). Closest: `motion/tween`, `camera/push-in`. Proposal: motion/camera (new): one call moves listed layers with depth factors.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/camera-push-in`, batch 3.1): Layers listed with depth factors (background 0.2, mid 0.55, subject 1.0, foreground 1.6). Push: each layer scales about the frame centre from 1 to 1 + 0.12 x depth (subject 1.12, foreground ~1.2) over 1600 ms easeInOutCubic. Flat mode = all layers equal (a zoom).
- [`cam-pullout`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=cam-pullout) **Pull Out** (Camera; dom; 1600 ms; easeInOutCubic; partial, high). Closest: `motion/tween`, `camera/push-in`. Proposal: motion/camera (new): one call moves listed layers with depth factors.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/camera-pull-out`, batch 3.1): Pull out: each layer scale 1 + 0.3 x depth -> 1 (subject 1.3 -> 1) over 1600 ms easeInOutCubic.
- [`cam-truck`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=cam-truck) **Truck** (Camera; dom; 2000 ms; easeInOutSine; partial, high). Closest: `motion/tween`. Proposal: motion/camera (new): one call moves listed layers with depth factors.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/camera-truck`, batch 3.1): Truck: layer x 0 -> -120 px x depth (near -120, far -24 at depth 0.2) over 2000 ms easeInOutSine.
- [`cam-pedestal`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=cam-pedestal) **Pedestal** (Camera; dom; 2000 ms; easeInOutSine; partial, high). Closest: `motion/tween`. Proposal: motion/camera (new): one call moves listed layers with depth factors.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/camera-pedestal`, batch 3.1): Pedestal: layer y 0 -> -80 px x depth (far layer x 0.2) over 2000 ms easeInOutSine.
- [`cam-vertigo`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=cam-vertigo) **Dolly Zoom (Vertigo)** (Camera; dom; 2000 ms; easeInOutCubic; partial, medium). Closest: `camera/dolly-zoom`. Proposal: motion/camera (new), type vertigo.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/dolly-zoom`, batch 3.1): Subject layer keeps its screen size while the camera moves z 0 -> +300 (perspective 1100 px) and background layers scale 1 -> 0.6, 2000 ms easeInOutCubic; per-layer scale compensation from each layer's distance. camera/dolly-zoom solves the focal length per frame but only on its own content. (The site's canvas demo scales every layer by 1 - 0.6 x depth, so its subject shrinks too: follow the recipe.)
- [`cam-crane`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=cam-crane) **Crane / Jib** (Camera; dom; 2000 ms; easeInOutCubic; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/tween`. Proposal: motion/camera (new), type crane.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/camera-crane`, batch 3.1): Crane up: the camera rises 100 px, so layers move down by 100 px x depth, while the stage tilts 8 deg to look down (rotateX on a perspective parent) with a 10 % push, 2000 ms easeInOutCubic. Demo: camera y +30 -> -40 with a push 1 -> 1.1 over 2.4 s ease-in-out, no tilt drawn.
- [`cam-whip`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=cam-whip) **Whip** (Camera; dom; 450 ms; cubic-bezier(.9,0,.1,1); partial, medium). Closest: `transition/whip-pan-cut`. Proposal: motion/camera (new), type whip (within one scene).
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/camera-whip`, batch 3.1): Within one scene the camera swings x by 200 px at depth 1 (each layer by its depth factor) over 450 ms cubic-bezier(.9,0,.1,1); smear = 3 trailing copies 12 px apart at opacity 0.25 x sin(pi u) (u = swing progress), so it shows only mid-swing; landing frame sharp. Demo swings out at 0.9 s and back at 2.3 s.
- [`cam-parallax`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=cam-parallax) **Parallax Layers** (Camera; dom; 4000 ms; linear; partial, high). Closest: `motion/tween`. Proposal: motion/camera (new): one call moves listed layers with depth factors.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/camera-parallax`, batch 3.1): Parallax: one travel D; foreground moves D x 1.0, mid D x 0.5, background D x 0.2, linear constant speed over the shot (demo: 4 s, D = +-60 px as a sine loop).
- [`cam-follow`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=cam-follow) **Follow with Lookahead** (Camera; dom; 600 ms; spring (lookahead); partial, low). Closest: `motion/spring-track`, `cursor/zoom-follow`. Proposal: motion/camera (new), type follow.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/camera-follow`, batch 3.1): Camera x target = subject x + subject velocity x 0.47 s (look-ahead ~14 frames, capped +-46 px), followed by a spring (stiffness 120, damping 20); background layers move at their depth factor (far 0.4). The demo approximates the spring with an 80 ms lag + a 46 tanh(v / 120) lead on a subject swinging +-220 px every 3 s.
- [`catalog-ui-spatial-infinite-canvas-jump`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-spatial-infinite-canvas-jump) **Infinite Canvas Jump** (Spatial UI & Depth; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, high). Closest: `flow/ui-focus-zoom`, `cursor/zoom-follow`. Proposal: camera/fly-to (new) or flow/ui-focus-zoom + hop mode (zoom dips while panning).
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/canvas-hop`, batch 3.1): Canvas with frames A and B 520 px apart (110 px vertically), dotted grid and filler cards. The camera centre moves A to B over 1.8 s power2.inOut (0.45-2.25 s) while zoom = 2 × 0.21^(sin(π u)) (u = eased progress): 2× at both ends, 0.42× at the midpoint, so it pulls out, travels and pushes in. Optional minimap (78x38) with the viewport rectangle. Demo fades to dark 3.55-3.85 s to loop.
- [`catalog-ui-spatial-camera-dolly-ui`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-spatial-camera-dolly-ui) **Camera Dolly UI** (Spatial UI & Depth; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, high). Closest: `camera/push-in`, `card/depth-stack`. Proposal: camera/dolly-layers (new): film layers given a z depth, camera z move.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/dolly-layers`, batch 3.1): Two planes: FRONT (120x74, accent) at depth z 0 offset (+44, +26), BACK (170x110 card) at z 260 offset (−40, −24) (focal 360, so BACK starts at 0.58 scale). Camera z 0 → 110 over 1.5 s power2.inOut (0.4-1.9 s), back 3.0-3.9 s. Each layer scale = 360 / (360 + z − camera z) and its offset from the centre scales the same way: FRONT ×1.44, BACK ×1.22. Dashed outlines show the start sizes.
- [`catalog-ui-spatial-parallax-inspector`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-spatial-parallax-inspector) **Parallax Inspector** (Spatial UI & Depth; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, high). Closest: `camera/scroll-story`, `camera/focus-rack`. Proposal: camera/dolly-layers (new) sideways pan: each layer moves by its depth factor.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/parallax-pan`, batch 3.1): Three layers BG / CONTENT / UI move ×0.25 / ×0.6 / ×1 of a sideways swing: 0 → +44 px (0.35-1.05 s), hold, → −44 px (1.85-2.75 s), hold, → 0 (3.45-4.15 s), power2.inOut (700-900 ms per move). BG = picture card 188x104, CONTENT = text card 160x84, UI = accent panel 120x64; dashed outlines mark rest positions; a bar readout per layer (optional).

### ease aliases (eases.js): NEW · high · - · ae keyframes · 9 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: other:gsap (2).

- [`e-whip`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=e-whip) **Whip** (Easing; dom; 500 ms; cubic-bezier(.9,0,.1,1); missing, low). Closest: none. Proposal: ease alias whip in eases.js.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/ease-whip`, batch 3.3): cubic-bezier(.9, 0, .1, 1), 500 ms (site motion_ms): a near-vertical peak. For slams and transitions; add a smear (motion blur) to the fast middle. Writable now as custom(M0,0 C .9,0 .1,1 1,1); proposed alias whip. Site demo: 1200 ms across 114 px beside the curve graph and a 12-frame spacing ruler (guides, not ported).
- [`e-glide`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=e-glide) **Glide** (Easing; dom; 300 ms; cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1); missing, low). Closest: none. Proposal: ease alias glide in eases.js.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/ease-glide`, batch 3.3): cubic-bezier(.4, 0, .2, 1) (the older Material standard), 300 ms (site motion_ms). Gentle; near text and for UI explanations, a premium default. Writable now as custom(M0,0 C .4,0 .2,1 1,1); proposed alias glide. Site demo: 1200 ms across 114 px beside the curve graph and a 12-frame spacing ruler (guides, not ported).
- [`e-md3`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=e-md3) **Material 3 Standard** (Easing; dom; 300 ms; cubic-bezier(.2,0,0,1); missing, low). Closest: none. Proposal: ease alias md3.standard in eases.js.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/ease-md3-standard`, batch 3.3): cubic-bezier(.2, 0, 0, 1), 300 ms (site motion_ms). The Material standard; default for corporate / product UI. Writable now as custom(M0,0 C .2,0 0,1 1,1); proposed alias md3.standard. Site demo: 1200 ms across 114 px beside the curve graph and a 12-frame spacing ruler (guides, not ported).
- [`e-md3e`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=e-md3e) **Material 3 Emphasized** (Easing; dom; 500 ms; cubic-bezier(.05,.7,.1,1); missing, low). Closest: none. Proposal: ease alias md3.emphasized in eases.js.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/ease-md3-emphasized`, batch 3.3): cubic-bezier(.05, .7, .1, 1) (emphasized decelerate), 500 ms (site motion_ms), for emphasized entrances and hero moments. Writable now as custom(M0,0 C .05,.7 .1,1 1,1); proposed alias md3.emphasized. Site demo: 1200 ms across 114 px beside the curve graph and a 12-frame spacing ruler (guides, not ported).
- [`e-md3a`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=e-md3a) **Material 3 Accelerate** (Easing; dom; 200 ms; cubic-bezier(.3,0,1,1); missing, low). Closest: none. Proposal: ease alias md3.accelerate in eases.js.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/ease-md3-accelerate`, batch 3.3): cubic-bezier(.3, 0, 1, 1), 200 ms (site motion_ms), for exits: accelerates to the very end and disappears. Writable now as custom(M0,0 C .3,0 1,1 1,1); proposed alias md3.accelerate. Site demo: 1200 ms across 114 px beside the curve graph and a 12-frame spacing ruler (guides, not ported).
- [`e-apple`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=e-apple) **Apple HIG** (Easing; dom; 350 ms; ease; missing, low). Closest: none. Proposal: ease alias css.ease in eases.js.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/ease-apple`, batch 3.3): cubic-bezier(.25, .1, .25, 1) (CSS 'ease'), 350 ms (site motion_ms). Site rule: same as CSS ease; pick it on purpose, not as an unexamined default. GSAP has no 'ease' name: write custom(M0,0 C .25,.1 .25,1 1,1); proposed alias css.ease. Site demo: 1200 ms across 114 px beside the curve graph and a 12-frame spacing ruler (guides, not ported).
- [`e-slowmo`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=e-slowmo) **SlowMo** (Easing; dom; 1200 ms; SlowMo(0.7, 0.7); partial, medium · origin other:gsap). Closest: `motion/extended-arrival`. Proposal: ease alias slowmo in eases.js.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/ease-slowmo`, batch 3.3): SlowMo(0.7, 0.7) as one ease: the first 15 % of the time covers about 40 % of the distance (fast, easing into the slow part), the middle 70 % moves linearly through only about 21 % (value 0.35 + 0.3 p), the last 15 % covers the remaining ~40 %, accelerating out; 1200 ms. For titles passing through the frame; the key moment sits in the slow middle. Site demo approximates it with cubic-bezier(.1,.65,.9,.35).
- [`e-expo-scale`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=e-expo-scale) **ExpoScaleEase** (Easing; dom; 1800 ms; ExpoScaleEase(1, 8); partial, high · origin other:gsap). Closest: `camera/push-in`. Proposal: ease alias expoScale in eases.js.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/ease-expo-scale`, batch 3.3): Zoom scale 1 → 8 over 1800 ms interpolated in log space: scale(p) = start × (end / start)^p, p linear or eased, so every doubling takes the same time; as a 0→1 ease for a scale tween 1→8: e(p) = (8^p − 1) / 7. Proposed alias expoScale(start, end[, inner ease]). Site demo: linear vs exponential zoom side by side on a small square.
- [`t-ramp`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=t-ramp) **Speed Ramp into a Hit** (Timing & Rhythm; dom; 1200 ms; speed ramp; partial, medium). Closest: `transition/beat-freeze-cut`, `motion/extended-arrival`. Proposal: ease alias ramp in eases.js.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/speed-ramp`, batch 3.6): Recipe: over a 1200 ms move into a hit, speed 100 % until 300 ms before the impact, eased down to 25 % over those 300 ms, the impact frame on the downbeat, then back to 100 %. Site demo: rate 100 % until 0.8 s, linear ramp to 25 % over 500 ms, 50 ms at 25 % (impact), then 130 % at once; a dot integrates the rate (130 px/s at 100 %) with a 6-copy trail. As an ease / time-remap: ramp(slow 0.25, window 0.3 s, impact time).

### effect/ring-pulse: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 9 motion(s)

- [`pulse-focus`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=pulse-focus) **Focus Pulse** (Scale; canvas; 680 ms; easeOutCubic; partial, high). Closest: `motion/tween`, `cursor/press-ripple`. Proposal: effect/ping-ring (new).
  Spec (corrected; import as `effect/focus-pulse`, batch 3.4): Ring centred on an icon of radius 12 px: ring radius 16 px × scale 0.5 → 2.4, opacity 0.8 → 0, 2 px stroke, 680 ms power2.out (easeOutCubic); the icon bumps 1 → 1.06 → 1 (sine) over the first 240 ms. 500 ms after the ring starts a tooltip ('New') fades in and rises 4 px in 200 ms above it. Once; the centre never moves.
- [`focus-halo`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=focus-halo) **Focus Halo** (Attention & Emphasis; canvas; 900 ms; easeOutCubic; partial, high). Closest: `motion/tween`, `flow/ui-focus-zoom`. Proposal: effect/ping-ring (new).
  Spec (verified; import as `effect/focus-halo`, batch 3.1): Ring on the target's outline (radius R): scale 0.75 → 1.9, opacity 0.7 → 0, 2.5 px stroke plus a 10 px wide soft copy at 35 % of that opacity, 900 ms power2.out (easeOutCubic); the target bumps 1 → 1.04 → 1 (sine) over the first 450 ms. Centre still; neighbours untouched.
- [`radial-flash`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=radial-flash) **Radial Flash** (Light & Glow; canvas; 560 ms; easeOutExpo; partial, high). Closest: `logo/logo-sting`, `effect/beat-accent`. Proposal: effect/ping-ring (new) + rays, flash.
  Spec (verified; import as `effect/radial-flash`, batch 3.2): Hit at t0: core brightness 100 → 220 % → 100 (sine bump^0.7 over 320 ms) with an additive soft glow; the star pops: scale 1 + 0.14·e^(−7 t)·sin(22 t), first half-wave only. From 60 ms a ring: radius 0.2 → 2.3 × 40 px, opacity 0.9 → 0, stroke 4 → 1 px, 500 ms expo.out. 12 rays in two colours move out (30→60 to 36→88 px) and fade over 350 ms expo.out.
- [`catalog-ui-spatial-spatial-audio-locator`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-spatial-spatial-audio-locator) **Spatial Audio Locator** (Spatial UI & Depth; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; missing, low). Closest: none. Proposal: effect/sonar (new).
  Spec (verified; import as `effect/sonar-locator`, batch 3.1): Listener at the centre with range rings r32 / 58 / 84 and a crosshair. Source dot r7 moves (228,52) → (86,78) at 2.0-2.6 s and back by 3.6-4.2 s (power2.inOut). Every 0.6 s a 1.5 px accent ring starts at the source's position at that moment, radius grows 90 px/s, fades over 1.4 s. A bearing arc (r20, ±0.45 rad, 4 px) turns toward the source's next stop on a spring k120 d18 and thickens to 7 px / brightens when a ring reaches the listener.
- [`catalog-game-hud-minimap-ping`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-hud-minimap-ping) **Minimap Ping** (Game HUD; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `map/pin-route`, `shape/pop`. Proposal: effect/ring-pulse (new).
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/minimap-ping`, batch 3.7): px @320x200. Static map card 148 x 148 (r 14); pin pops at 0.42 s (300 ms back.out). Rings from the pin: radius 4 → 34 in 1.0 s outCubic, opacity 1 → 0, stroke 2, accent; starts 0.45, 0.73, 1.40, 1.68 s (two 280 ms apart, the pair repeated 950 ms later). Distance label fades in 0.5-0.7 s; pin and label leave 2.35-2.55 s (pin in-cubic). The map never moves.
- [`catalog-game-hud-objective-marker`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-hud-objective-marker) **Objective Marker** (Game HUD; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `map/pin-route`, `motion/inertial-bounce`. Proposal: effect/ring-pulse (new) + flat ellipse ratio.
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/objective-marker`, batch 3.7): px @320x200. Diamond marker 22 x 30 drops y −24 → 98 by spring k150 d15 from 0.3 s, then bobs ±2 px (sine 5 rad/s) eased in over 1.3-1.6 s. Ground point 52 px below: shadow ellipse 6 x 2 → 16 x 5, opacity 25 → 60 % with nearness; two flat rings (height 0.32 × width) radius 6 → 32, stroke 2, 700 ms outCubic, at 0.58 and 0.74 s; static ground ring 15 x 5 + dashed leader fade in 0.6-0.8 s; distance label. Exit up 140 px, 350 ms in-cubic at 2.35 s.
- [`catalog-game-combat-parry-window`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-combat-parry-window) **Parry Window** (Combat & Hit FX; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; missing, low). Closest: none. Proposal: effect/ring-pulse (new) direction=in.
  Spec (corrected; import as `effect/parry-timing-ring`, batch 3.7): px @320x200. Timing ring (stroke 2, accent) shrinks radius 54 → 16 linearly over 700 ms (0.3-1.0 s), opacity 0.3 → 1, onto a dashed target circle r 16. Contact at 1.0 s: flash ring 16 → 40 (stroke 3) in 350 ms outCubic; shield pushed 6 px (250 ms half-sine); 9-dot burst to 20 px (300 ms). The projectile comes in at ~269 px/s and leaves up-right at 420 px/s x, 90 px/s up (~1.6×; site label says ×2.4) for 600 ms; 'PARRY' pops 300 ms back.out.
- [`catalog-game-reward-level-up`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-reward-level-up) **Level Up Burst** (Rewards & Loot; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `text/anime-roll`, `house/burst-pop`. Proposal: effect/ring-pulse (new) + effect/spark-burst (new).
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/level-up-burst`, batch 3.5): px @320x200. XP bar 100 x 6 fills 0.3-0.95 s inOut (blue, accent after the peak). Digit (50 px) rolls 7 → 8 vertically (56 px, clipped) in 200 ms back.out at 1.0 s. Peak 1.2 s: number scale 1.3 → 1 spring k300 d12; halo ring r 30 → 96 in 600 ms outCubic, stroke 4 → 1, fading; 24 seeded dots in 3 colours burst to 47-86 px over 800 ms; 'LEVEL UP!' pops at 1.25 s (300 ms back.out); bar drains in 300 ms outCubic. Loop fade 2.4-2.8 s.
- [`catalog-game-impact-fx-shockwave-type`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-impact-fx-shockwave-type) **Shockwave Typography** (Game Impact FX; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, high). Closest: `logo/logo-sting`, `text/headline-slam`. Proposal: effect/ring-pulse (new).
  Spec (verified; import as `text/shockwave-type`, batch 3.1): px @320x200. Word (50 px) scales 0 → 1.28 in 230 ms outCubic (0.35-0.58 s), then to 1 by spring k300 d16; shakes ±4 px x/y for 160 ms at the peak. Two flat rings (height 0.62 × width) radius 34 → 204 over ~1.2 s outCubic, starting 20 and 100 ms after the peak, opacity 1 − q², stroke 7 → 1 (accent) and 3.5 → 1 (light ink); 10 radial dashes fly out (inner end +30 px, outer +60 px) in 400 ms outCubic, fading. Exit 3.6-3.9 s back.in.

### transition/iris-reveal: EXTEND · high · effect · ae keyframes · 9 motion(s)

- [`ripple-reveal`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=ripple-reveal) **Ripple Reveal** (Effects & Transitions; canvas; 950 ms; easeOutCubic; partial, medium). Target revealed by a circle clip from the tap that grows with ring 1 (x1.05) and opacity 0 -> 1 from +80 to +700 ms easeOutCubic. Optional 5 px tap dot for 140 ms.
- [`svg-mask-reveal`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=svg-mask-reveal) **SVG Mask Reveal** (SVG & Path; canvas; 980 ms; easeOutCubic; partial, medium). Closest: `transition/iris-reveal`. Proposal: transition/iris-reveal + param solo (reveal one element, no outgoing scene).

Exit: radius back to 0 over 350 ms ease-in.
- [`tr-inset`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tr-inset) **Inset (clip-path)** (Transitions; dom; 500 ms; easeInOutCubic; partial, high). Closest: `transition/iris-reveal`. Proposal: transition/iris-reveal + param shape inset.
  Spec (verified; import as `transition/inset-reveal`, batch 3.1): Incoming scene clipped inset(50 %) -> inset(0) from the centre over 500 ms easeInOutCubic (demo: expo.out); outgoing still. Optional reverse (the outgoing closes to the centre).
- [`tr-clock`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tr-clock) **Clock Wipe** (Transitions; dom; 700 ms; easeInOutSine; partial, medium). Closest: `transition/showcase-mechanical`, `transition/iris-reveal`. Proposal: transition/iris-reveal + param shape clock.
  Spec (verified; import as `transition/clock-wipe`, batch 3.1): Pie clip centred on the frame, from 12 o'clock clockwise, sweep 0 -> 360 deg over 700 ms easeInOutSine, radius past the far corner; incoming scene inside the pie, outgoing still underneath. Use a polygon clip with a fixed vertex count. HF showcase-mechanical ships a Clock Wipe (Apache-2.0).
- [`tr-shapeiris`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tr-shapeiris) **Shaped Iris** (Transitions; dom; 700 ms; easeInCubic; partial, medium). Closest: `transition/iris-reveal`, `transition/showcase-radial`. Proposal: transition/iris-reveal + param shape (polygon: heart, diamond, keyhole, star).
  Spec (verified; import as `transition/shape-iris`, batch 3.1): Incoming scene clipped to a shape centred on the frame, scale 0 -> cover (until the shape clears every corner; ~7 x a heart 16 % of frame width) over 700 ms easeInCubic. Use a polygon clip (KI-03: clip-path path() fails in After Effects). HF showcase-radial ships a Diamond Iris.
- [`tr-flood`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tr-flood) **Colour Flood** (Transitions; dom; 800 ms; easeInOutCubic; partial, high). Closest: `transition/iris-reveal`, `transition/fade-through`. Proposal: transition/iris-reveal + param flood (a colour disc covers, swap, uncovers).
  Spec (verified; import as `transition/color-flood`, batch 3.1): A solid colour disc grows from a point (default 12 % x / 80 % y) to the farthest-corner radius over 400 ms easeInCubic, covering the outgoing scene; cut to the incoming scene underneath at 400 ms; the disc shrinks back to its point 400-800 ms easeOutCubic. 800 ms total.
- [`catalog-ui-spatial-focus-portal`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-spatial-focus-portal) **Focus Portal** (Spatial UI & Depth; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, medium).   Spec (verified; import as `transition/focus-portal`, batch 3.1): Circle clip at the focus point opens radius 10 → 72 px in 0.7 s power4.out (quint) at 0.45 s; a 2 px accent rim follows its edge. Inside: plain background and a card 108x80 scaling 0.6 → 1 and fading in over 0.7 s quint, starting 120 ms after the opening. Close: content 3.0-3.4 s, then the circle 3.1-3.55 s, both power2.in. The grid of cards outside stays still.
- [`catalog-ui-material-frost-melt`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-material-frost-melt) **Frost Melt Unlock** (Material & Surface Motion; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `transition/iris-reveal`, `effect/ink-bleed-reveal`. Proposal: transition/iris-reveal + params invert (the cover shrinks to the centre) and rough edge.

Its round mask (radius 112 → 0, vertical × 0.78, edge noise ±16 % drifting slowly) shrinks from the edges to the centre over 1.65 s power1.inOut (0.45-2.1 s) with a thin light rim (1.5 px, 70 %) on the melting edge; the unlocked content (accent lock, UNLOCKED) shows beneath; the lock is the last thing covered. The frost returns by a fade 3.3-3.8 s.
- [`tx-shape-mask-cut`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-shape-mask-cut) **Shape-Mask Transition** (Mask & Clip; text; 600 ms; ease-in (cubic); partial, high). Closest: `transition/iris-reveal`, `transition/shader-sdf-iris`. Proposal: transition/iris-reveal + params shape (star / polygon / SVG path) and rotate.

Demo: the star grows from 0, next scene = accent background with the second title; cut 1.2–1.8 s, back 3.0–3.6 s; loop 4.8 s.

### motion/squash-stretch: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 8 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: other:gsap (1).

- [`heavy-stop`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=heavy-stop) **Heavy Stop** (Movement; canvas; 680 ms; easeOutQuart; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/inertial-bounce`, `hand/hit-stop`. Proposal: motion/squash-stretch (new) mode land.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/heavy-stop`, batch 3.7): x 0 → 182 px in 462 ms power3.out (easeOutQuart), back to 175 by 680 ms power2.inOut (7 px overshoot) (recipe values; demo 202 / 195 px). From 320 ms the block squashes along the travel (ease-in) to scaleX 0.82 / scaleY 1.18 at arrival, then a damped wobble k = e^(−11 t)·cos(26 t) back to 1/1 (scale 1 ∓ 0.18 k, t in s after arrival). Pivot: bottom leading corner (100 % 100 %), at a stop line. 6 dust dots puff over 550 ms from 400 ms.
- [`axis-stretch`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=axis-stretch) **Axis Stretch** (Scale; canvas; 700 ms; easeInOutBack; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/tween`, `motion/echo-trail`. Proposal: motion/squash-stretch (new) mode stretch.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/speed-stretch`, batch 3.4): Ball x 0 → 200 px (demo; recipe 10 → 175) in 700 ms back.inOut(1.7), back again in 700 ms after a 550 ms hold. Its scale follows its own speed: scaleX = 1 + 0.7 q, scaleY = 1 − 0.35 q, q = speed / 640 px/s on the 320 px demo canvas (≈ speed / peak speed, clamped 0–1): about 170 / 65 % mid-travel, round at rest and at the back-ease turns. Optional 4 ghosts 30 ms apart at opacity 0.025–0.1.
- [`bounce-land`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=bounce-land) **Bounce Landing** (Bounce & Reaction; canvas; 700 ms; easeOutBounce; partial, medium). Closest: `text/land-in`, `hand/hit-stop`. Proposal: motion/squash-stretch (new) mode bounce.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/bounce-land`, batch 3.5): Drop from 106 px (demo; recipe 130) to the floor: fall 266 ms (parabola), then bounces 52 / 22 / 8 px high lasting 225 / 142 / 86 ms (parabolic arcs), last contact at 719 ms (bounce.out timing). At each contact squash scaleY by 32 / 20 / 10 / 4 % (scaleX = 1/scaleY) as a 100 ms triangle centred on the contact, origin bottom. Drifts 140 px right over 780 ms (linear); shadow shrinks with height.
- [`double-bounce`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=double-bounce) **Double Bounce** (Bounce & Reaction; canvas; 820 ms; easeOutBounce; partial, medium). Closest: `text/land-in`, `hand/hit-stop`. Proposal: motion/squash-stretch (new) mode bounce.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/double-bounce`, batch 3.5): y −100 → 0 in 287 ms (fall, ease-in), up to −55 and down in 287 ms, up to −25 and down in 246 ms (parabolas), done at 820 ms; squash 30 / 18 / 8 % at the three contacts (scaleX = 1/scaleY, 100 ms triangles, origin bottom). After the last contact a check mark draws inside (260 ms) and a ring pings (+18 px, 480 ms).
- [`waypoint-hop`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=waypoint-hop) **Waypoint Hop** (Movement; canvas; 860 ms; easeOutCubic; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/anime`, `map/pin-route`. Proposal: motion/squash-stretch (new) mode hop.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/waypoint-hop`, batch 3.7): A marker hops across 4 points ~67 px apart: each hop 250 ms (x power2.out, y = −24 to −28·sin(π q) px arc), then a 50 ms ground beat with landing squash scaleY 0.92 (120 ms sine bump); each reached point pops (scale back.out, 200 ms) and a progress line fills behind. Total 860–900 ms.
- [`squash`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=squash) **Squash & Stretch** (Principles; dom; 550 ms; easeInQuad; partial, high). Closest: `hand/hit-stop`, `text/land-in`. Proposal: motion/squash-stretch (new).
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/squash-stretch`, batch 3.4): Recipe: ball falls in 300 ms power1.in (easeInQuad), stretching with speed up to scaleY 1.2 / scaleX 0.85; at contact scaleY 0.75 / scaleX 1.33 (volume kept, origin bottom) for 2–4 frames; rises 300–550 ms power1.out back to 1/1. Site demo: endless parabolic bounce 112 px high, 1100 ms per bounce, stretch scaleY 1 + 0.22·v² (v = speed 0–1, scaleX = 1/scaleY), contact pose 0.72 / 1.36 for the 110 ms around the floor; an undeformed ghost alongside.
- [`e-custombounce`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=e-custombounce) **CustomBounce with Squash** (Easing; dom; 1200 ms; CustomBounce(strength 0.7); partial, medium · origin other:gsap). Closest: `text/land-in`, `hand/hit-stop`. Proposal: motion/squash-stretch (new) mode bounce.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/bounce-squash`, batch 3.3): Recipe: first contact at 400 ms; each bounce keeps 70 % of the previous height (strength 0.7) and lasts √0.7 of the previous, ~1200 ms in all; squash only on the 1–2 contact frames, weaker each bounce, origin bottom; slight stretch in the fast fall. Site demo: bounce.out over 1600 ms, 112 px drop (contacts at 36 / 73 / 91 %), squash = 0.5·(1 − p) within 4 % of the floor: scaleY 0.68 / 0.86 / 0.95, scaleX 1 + the same amount.
- [`catalog-ui-material-clay-icon-morph`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-material-clay-icon-morph) **Clay Morph Icon** (Material & Surface Motion; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `shape/anime-morph`, `house/hop`. Proposal: motion/squash-hop (new), combined with shape/anime-morph.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/squash-hop`, batch 3.5): Soft square r27 on the floor (hops at 0.3 / 2.4 s). Wind-up squash scaleY 1 → 0.72 in 0.3 s power2.out (scaleX inverse); hop 0.66 s parabola, apex 62 px, stretch 1.14 at take-off (gone by 40 % of the air time); in the air it spins 90° and morphs square → circle (both power2.inOut; morph 0.36-0.92 s after the wind-up starts ≈ 9-94 % of the hop); landing squash 0.8, spring k320 d11 back to 1; shadow shrinks with height. Second hop morphs back.

### text/captions: EXTEND · high · block · ae keyframes · 8 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: budoux (1).

- [`tx-pop-on-subtitle`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-pop-on-subtitle) **Pop-On Subtitle** (Captions & Subtitles; text; 4200 ms; none (cut); partial, high). Closest: `text/captions`. Proposal: text/captions + param mode: subtitle (cut cues) + .srt timing rules in kit.py transcribe.
  Spec (verified; import as `text/subtitle-pop-on`, batch 3.3): Cues cut on and off, no motion. Rules: ≤ 42 chars per line, ≤ 2 lines, ≤ 17–20 chars/s, each cue ≥ 20 frames at 24 fps (5/6 s) and ≤ 7 s, ≥ 2-frame gap, times snapped to frames. Demo: two sentences as cues 0.35–1.95 s and 2.03–3.6 s; text 18 px bold, white with a 3.5 px dark outline (no box), centred in the lower third. Loop 4.2 s.
- [`tx-roll-up-caption`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-roll-up-caption) **Roll-Up Caption** (Captions & Subtitles; text; 270 ms; easeOutCubic; partial, low). Closest: `text/captions`. Proposal: text/captions + param mode: rollup.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/roll-up-caption`, batch 3.3): 3-row window (row 1.45 em, text 17 px bold, left-aligned), clipped. A new line starts every 0.85 s from 0.4 s; 0.27 s before each new line all rows shift up one row, power2.out (cubic-bezier .33,1,.68,1), and the top row fades as it leaves. The new line paints on in the bottom row left → right over 0.5 s, char by char; each line has a dark box behind its shown chars (5 px pad). Clear 4.55–4.75 s. Loop 5 s.
- [`tx-live-caption-block`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-live-caption-block) **Live Caption Block** (Captions & Subtitles; text; 5400 ms; none (cut); partial, low). Closest: `text/captions`. Proposal: text/captions + param mode: subtitle (cut cues) + .srt timing rules in kit.py transcribe.
  Spec (verified; import as `text/live-caption-block`, batch 3.3): 2-line block (≤ 30 chars per line, 17 px bold) on an opaque dark box 268 × 50 px (radius 3) near the bottom. Blocks are replaced wholesale on a hard cut, no scroll or fade: demo 0.3–2.6 s and 2.6–4.9 s. Pacing: about 270 chars per minute measured on live TV (film guideline 240 per minute), so a block's time = its chars / 270 × 60 s. Optional LIVE tag (red dot + mono label) top-left. Loop 5.4 s.
- [`tx-phrase-line-break`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-phrase-line-break) **Phrase-Aware Line Break** (Captions & Subtitles; text; 550 ms; easeOutExpo; partial, low · origin budoux). Closest: `text/captions`. Proposal: text/captions + param break: phrase (no split inside compound words, balance 2 lines).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/phrase-line-break`, batch 3.3): Rule: break at the phrase / clause boundary nearest the middle of the line instead of a fixed character count (study: 74 % vs 21 % preferred); for Vietnamese keep multi-syllable words (e.g. 'thành phố') together via a word list. Motion: the caption (bold, ≤ 20 px in the demo) starts as one line; from 0.45 s each char glides to its two-line slot and resizes, 0.55 s expo.out (cubic-bezier .16,1,.3,1), stagger spread over 0.5 s; merges back 3.85–4.4 s. Loop 4.6 s.
- [`tx-jp-subtitle-timing`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-jp-subtitle-timing) **Japanese Subtitle 4 cps** (Japanese; text; 2000 ms; none (cut); partial, low). Closest: `text/captions`. Proposal: text/captions + param mode: subtitle (cut cues) + .srt timing rules in kit.py transcribe.
  Spec (verified; import as `text/subtitle-cps-timing`, batch 3.5): Cue time = max(0.5 s, chars ÷ 4) (half-width chars count 0.5); ≤ 13 chars per line, ≤ 2 lines; 0.12 s gap (3 frames at 24 fps) between cues; schedule scaled to fit if longer; '、' → space, final '。' dropped, inner '。' → full-width space. Text 15 px, white with a 3.5 px dark outline, centred at the bottom of the picture; cuts only. Demo: cues from 0.35 s. Loop 4.6 s. For Vietnamese use a Latin-script rate (≈ 17 chars/s).
- [`tx-vertical-subtitle`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-vertical-subtitle) **Vertical Subtitle** (Japanese; text; 4200 ms; none (cut); partial, low). Closest: `text/captions`. Proposal: text/captions + param mode: subtitle (cut cues) + .srt timing rules in kit.py transcribe + position switch.
  Spec (verified; import as `text/subtitle-reposition`, batch 3.5): Subtitle (14 px, white, 3.5 px dark outline) at the bottom from 0.3 s. At 1.4 s burned-in text appears in the bottom band of the picture. At 1.95 s the subtitle cuts, no transition, to the alternative place: Japanese → vertical columns at the right edge (≤ 11 chars per column, ≤ 2 columns, ≤ 15 px, top 9 px, right 14 px); Latin set sideways in a column (for Vietnamese films: the top of the frame instead). Off at 3.75 s. Loop 4.2 s.
- [`tx-cinema-handwritten-subtitle`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-cinema-handwritten-subtitle) **Handwritten-style Film Subtitle** (Japanese; text; 4400 ms; none (cut); partial, low). Closest: `text/captions`. Proposal: text/captions + param mode: subtitle (cut cues) + .srt timing rules in kit.py transcribe.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/handwritten-subtitle`, batch 3.5): Cuts in / out: duration clamp(chars ÷ 4, 1, 1.7) s, 0.25 s gap, from 0.35 s; 21 px weight 500, spacing 0.04 em, near the bottom. Per glyph, seeded: rotation ±4°, scaleX 0.86–0.98, scaleY 0.92–1.08, offset ±0.7 / ±1.6 px, skewX ±3.4°, plus a dark copy at (0.6, 0.8) px 70 %; dakuten redrawn as two bold strokes. Film look: 14 px letterbox bars, grain specks and a ±0.4 px gate weave changing every 24 fps frame. Loop 4.4 s.
- [`tx-telop-comment-follow`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-telop-comment-follow) **Comment-Follow Telop** (Broadcast & Telop; text; 167 ms; easeOutBack; partial, medium). Closest: `text/captions`. Proposal: text/captions + param speakers (outline colour per speaker).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/speaker-color-captions`, batch 3.5): One line at a time (24 px heavy, ≤ 2 lines, same place for all): ink fill, 0.3 em outer stroke in the speaker's colour, dark (2, 2) px shadow, a small badge (8 px circle, speaker letter) at its left. Each line pops 0.1 s after its turn starts: scale 0.85 → 1 in 5 frames at 30 fps back.out(1.7), opaque at a third of it; holds ≥ chars ÷ 4 s; fades out over 0.15 s ending 0.13 s before the next. Demo 3 lines × 2.2 s.

### data/chart-story: EXTEND · high · block · ae keyframes · 6 motion(s)

- [`catalog-data-story-threshold-wave`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-data-story-threshold-wave) **Threshold Alarm Wave** (Data Storytelling; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `data/chart-story`, `data/mk-line-graph`. Proposal: data/chart-story + param threshold.
  Spec (verified; import as `data/threshold-alert`, batch 3.2): Line (7 points) draws left -> right linearly 300-2400 ms with a head dot + value label. Dashed threshold line (5/4 dash, yellow) at value 60; the line past the crossing is red (clip from the crossing x). When the head reaches the crossing: dot pops 300 ms back.out, 3 rings radius 5 -> 39 px, 1 s easeOutCubic, 280 ms apart, opacity 1 -> 0; tag 'over' fades in +150 to +400 ms. Fade out 3600-3950.
- [`catalog-data-story-confidence-fog`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-data-story-confidence-fog) **Forecast Confidence Fog** (Data Storytelling; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `data/chart-story`. Proposal: data/chart-story + param forecast (dashed continuation + widening band).
  Spec (verified; import as `data/forecast-band`, batch 3.2): Observed line (5 points) draws 300-1300 ms easeInOut, end dot. 'Today' divider + labels fade 1200-1500. Forecast: dashed (5/4) curve from the last point draws 1500-2800 ms easeOutCubic; behind it a band whose half-width grows linearly from 0 at today to +-34 px at the end, as 4 nested bands (100 / 72 / 46 / 22 % width) at 11 % opacity each, revealed in step with the forecast. Width bracket fades 2600-2900.
- [`catalog-data-story-delta-split`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-data-story-delta-split) **Comparison Delta Split** (Data Storytelling; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, high). Closest: `data/chart-story`, `data/animated-bar-chart`. Proposal: data/chart-story + param delta (compare two bars, highlight the difference, +N label).
  Spec (verified; import as `data/delta-compare`, batch 3.2): Two bars on one baseline at 2 px per unit: A 40 (80 px) grows 300-1100 ms, B 64 (128 px) 400-1200 ms, easeOutExpo, labels count with them. A dashed guide from A's top extends to B 1400-1800 easeOutCubic; B's top 48 px (the delta) turns accent 1700-2000; a bracket beside B grows 1800-2150 easeOutCubic; '+24' pops 2100-2450 back.out. Exit 3500-3850 ease-in.
- [`catalog-data-story-milestone-firefly`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-data-story-milestone-firefly) **Progress Milestone Firefly** (Data Storytelling; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `data/chart-story`, `data/mk-progress-stat`, `flow/onboarding-stepper-flow`. Proposal: data/chart-story + param milestones (progress type: dots light when passed, glowing head).
  Spec (corrected; import as `data/milestone-progress`, batch 3.2): Bar 8 px tall fills 0 -> 75 % over 400-2400 ms easeInOut. Milestone dots (r 6) at 0/25/50/75/100 % turn accent the frame the fill passes them. Head: glowing accent dot (r 7, 14 px glow) with a background-colour core (r 3) at the fill end, % tag above; 8 trail sparks at 70 ms steps behind, shrinking, fading, +-5 px seeded y drift. At 2400 ms a ring 8 -> 34 px fades over 700 ms easeOutCubic. Back to 0 over 3500-3900 ms ease-in cubic.
- [`catalog-data-story-goal-projection`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-data-story-goal-projection) **Goal Projection Arc** (Data Storytelling; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `data/chart-story`. Proposal: data/chart-story + param forecast (dashed projection to a goal marker).
  Spec (verified; import as `data/goal-projection`, batch 3.2): Goal marker fixed from the start (ring r 8 + dot, dashed goal line, label). Actual line (5 points) draws 300-1300 ms easeInOut, end dot; divider + labels fade 1200-1500; dashed (5/4, yellow) projection curves from the last point to the goal 1500-2600 ms easeInOut with an open head dot; on arrival a ring 9 -> 29 px fades over 700 ms easeOutCubic. Fade 3600-3950.
- [`dv-scatter`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=dv-scatter) **Scatter Reveal** (Data Storytelling; dom; 600 ms; easeOutCubic; partial, medium). Closest: `data/chart-story`. Proposal: data/chart-story + type scatter.
  Spec (verified; import as `data/scatter-reveal`, batch 3.2): Axes; 24 points (r 4) rise from the x-axis to their y over 500 ms easeOutCubic, 30 ms apart (recipe 600 ms, 20 ms); then the regression line draws from its left end over 500-600 ms easeInOut in accent.

### motion/emphasis: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 5 motion(s)

- [`tilt-reaction`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tilt-reaction) **Tilt Reaction** (Rotation; canvas; 560 ms; easeOutBack; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/spring-track`, `motion/anime`. Proposal: motion/emphasis (new) style tilt.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/tilt-reaction`, batch 3.4): Rotation about the centre after a press on one side (sign follows the side): 0 → 16° in 120 ms power2.out, → −3° by 300 ms, → 0 by 480 ms (power2.inOut segments); contact shadow widens with /angle/ (+0.6 % per degree). Recipe variant: 0 → 18° (308 ms) → 0 (560 ms) back.out. Demo: the cursor taps the right side, then the left 1.1 s later.
- [`nudge`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=nudge) **Nudge** (Attention & Emphasis; canvas; 520 ms; easeOutBack; partial, high). Closest: `motion/anime`, `hand/shake`. Proposal: motion/emphasis (new) style nudge.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/nudge`, batch 3.1): x keys 0 → +9 px (180 ms) → −3 px (330 ms) → 0 (520 ms), sine.inOut between keys; only the target moves (a 'Next' button), once. Same preset family: styles nod, jelly, hop, tilt (one preset with a style param, like text/land-in).
- [`attention-bounce`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=attention-bounce) **Attention Bounce** (Attention & Emphasis; canvas; 620 ms; easeOutBounce; partial, high). Closest: `house/hop`, `motion/anime`. Proposal: motion/emphasis (new) style hop.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/attention-hop`, batch 3.1): Bell hops in place, origin bottom: y 0 → −16 px (260 ms) → +2 (430 ms) → 0 (620 ms), sine.inOut keys; pre-squash scaleY −4 % (sine bump from −80 to +20 ms around take-off), landing squash scaleY 0.92 (sine bump 360–560 ms), scaleX = 1/scaleY; contact shadow shrinks (−35 %) and fades with height. Once.
- [`jelly-emphasis`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=jelly-emphasis) **Jelly Emphasis** (Attention & Emphasis; canvas; 760 ms; easeOutElastic; partial, high). Closest: `motion/anime`, `hand/hit-stop`. Proposal: motion/emphasis (new) style jelly.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/jelly-emphasis`, batch 3.1): Origin bottom centre; scaleX / scaleY keys 1/1 → 1.25/0.78 (260 ms) → 0.88/1.12 (430 ms) → 1.06/0.96 (590 ms) → 1/1 (760 ms), sine.inOut between keys; contact shadow width follows scaleX. Once.
- [`icon-nod`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=icon-nod) **Icon Nod** (Attention & Emphasis; canvas; 620 ms; easeOutBack; partial, high). Closest: `motion/anime`, `motion/inertial-bounce`. Proposal: motion/emphasis (new) style nod.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/icon-nod`, batch 3.1): transform-origin 50 % 12 % (pivot near the top edge); rotation 0 → 16° (220 ms) → −5° (440 ms) → 0 (620 ms), sine.inOut between keys; once.

### data/odometer: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 4 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: number-flow (2), other:jquery-flapper (1).

- [`catalog-kinetic-type-mono-data-shuffle`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-kinetic-type-mono-data-shuffle) **Monospace Data Shuffle** (Numbers & Data; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, high). Closest: `data/number-wheel`, `data/countdown`. Proposal: data/odometer (new): roll a film number from A to B digit by digit, extra spins, stop stagger.
  Spec (corrected; import as `data/digit-odometer`, batch 3.2): Tabular mono digits (38 px), each in a clip window 1 digit wide × 1.37 em tall, row pitch 1.16 em; separators fixed. Digit k rolls forward ((to − from) mod 10) + 10·spins rows, spins 1 / 2 alternating; all start 0.4 s, digit k ends 1.1 + 0.12·k s, easeOutCubic (power2.out). Example 03,481 → 12,907. At 2.7 s each digit keeps rolling forward ((from − to) mod 10) + 10 rows back to the start value, digit k ends 3.2 + 0.1·k s; loop 4.2 s.
- [`tx-odometer-roll`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-odometer-roll) **Odometer Digit Roll** (Numbers & Data; text; 750 ms; ease-out; partial, high · origin number-flow). Closest: `data/countdown`, `data/number-wheel`. Proposal: data/odometer (new) (same as the mono data shuffle item).
  Spec (corrected; import as `data/odometer-roll`, batch 3.2): Per digit a 0–9 strip in a window one line tall (demo pitch 1.4 em) with a 0.25 em soft fade top / bottom; on a change each digit rolls to its new digit in 750 ms easeOutQuart (CSS cubic-bezier(.25,1,.5,1); GSAP power3.out), forward when the value rises, backward when it falls, tinting accent while rolling; added / removed chars fade over 350 ms ease-out while sliding with the roll; line-height 0.85–1. Demo: 1,024 → 2,048 at 0.4 s, back at 2.45 s; loop 4.4 s.
- [`tx-ticker-flash`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-ticker-flash) **Price Ticker Flash** (Numbers & Data; text; 600 ms; ease-out; partial, medium · origin number-flow). Closest: `data/countdown`. Proposal: data/odometer (new) + mode ticker (changed digits slide, green / red flash, arrow).
  Spec (corrected; import as `data/ticker-flash`, batch 3.2): Value changes at cues; only changed digits move: on a rise the new digit slides in from +0.3 em below (old out up), on a fall from above, 0.35 s easeOutCubic with fade; behind them a rounded flash (radius 6 px) green / red at 32 % fades over 0.6 s easeOutQuad, digits tinted the same, fading with it; a triangle up / down in that colour beside the number; tabular figures. Demo: +0.65 % at 0.4 s, down at 1.6 s, back at 2.8 s, small trend line; loop 3.6 s.
- [`tx-score-update`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-score-update) **Score Update** (Numbers & Data; text; 350 ms; ease-in-out; partial, low · origin other:jquery-flapper). Closest: `text/split-flap`, `data/countdown`. Proposal: data/odometer (new) + mode flip (scoreboard flip + two flashes).
  Spec (verified; import as `data/score-flip`, batch 3.2): On a score change the cell flips (old rotateX 0 → −90, new 90 → 0, 350 ms total ease-in-out; GSAP 0.175 s power1.in + 0.175 s power1.out) to the new number, then the cell background flashes twice (on 150 ms, off 150 ms, on 150 ms, off; text inverts while lit); names and separator stay still. Demo: card scoreboard, home scores at 0.7 s, away at 2.1 s, a small dot above the scoring cell for 1.1 s; reset 3.8 s; loop 4.2 s.

### motion/card-flip: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 3 motion(s)

- [`flip-card`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=flip-card) **Card Flip** (Rotation; dom; 760 ms; easeInOutCubic; partial, high). Closest: `transition/showcase-3d`, `text/land-in`. Proposal: motion/card-flip (new).
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/card-flip`, batch 3.4): Card rotates rotationY 0 → 180° in 760 ms power2.inOut (easeInOutCubic), hinge at the centre; site DOM demo: 58×42 px card, perspective 200 px in its transform, front 'F' in the accent, back 'B' in a darker accent (65 % mix). At 90° (380 ms) the front hides and the back shows — port as fold to 90°, swap, unfold from −90° (backface-visibility is unreliable in renders); the back reads unmirrored. Params: duration, axis x / y, perspective, turns.
- [`coin-spin`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=coin-spin) **Coin Spin** (Rotation; canvas; 900 ms; easeOutQuart; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/tween`, `logo/extruded-logo`. Proposal: motion/card-flip (new) + param turns.
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/coin-spin`, batch 3.4): rotateY 0 → 1080° (demo; recipe 900°, ending on the back) in 900 ms power3.out (easeOutQuart); perspective 320 px in the recipe, the demo draws it flat (width × /cos/). Front face (star) while cos(angle) ≥ 0, back face (ring) when < 0, swapped at each 90° (not via backface-visibility); a 7 px edge band drawn as 7·/sin/ between the faces; contact shadow scales 0.55–1 with /cos/. Ends face-on.
- [`catalog-ui-navigation-card-flip`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-navigation-card-flip) **Card Flip** (Navigation & Overlays; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `transition/showcase-3d`. Proposal: motion/card-flip (new): two-faced flip with the back face hidden.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/takayu-card-flip`, batch 3.4): Card 110x70 r8 with front (accent, 'FRONT', chip, number) and back (dark, stripe, 'BACK', 'DETAILS') faces. rotationY 0 → 180° over 0.7 s power2.inOut (0.4-1.1 s), back over 1.75-2.45 s after a hold; scaleY 1 + 0.06 × sin(angle) mid-flip; the face turned away is hidden (backface hidden, no mirrored text). AE: a 3D layer with two faces. (The demo also shows a wrong mirrored copy for comparison.)

### text/slot-roll: EXTEND · high · effect · ae keyframes · 3 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: react-bits (1).

- [`catalog-data-story-odometer-cascade`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-data-story-odometer-cascade) **Metric Odometer Cascade** (Data Storytelling; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, high). Closest: `data/number-wheel`, `text/slot-roll`, `data/countdown`. Proposal: text/slot-roll + params laps per digit (lower digits more), stop stagger, settle.
  Spec (verified; import as `data/odometer-cascade`, batch 3.2): Digits 1, 2, 8 + a fixed K. All reels start at 350 ms and roll (digit + laps x 10) steps, laps 1 / 2 / 3 from left to right, easeOutCubic, stopping at 1250 / 1750 / 2250 ms. On stop: settle wobble 0.2 step x sin(20 t) x e^(-10 t) and a ring flash (border grows 4 px and fades over 350 ms easeOutCubic). While fast: ghost copies +-6 px at ~14 % opacity.
- [`catalog-kinetic-type-glyph-slot-roll`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-kinetic-type-glyph-slot-roll) **Glyph Slot Roll** (Swap; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `text/slot-roll`, `text/anime-roll`. Proposal: text/slot-roll + params rows (seeded random glyphs before the target), stop stagger, overshoot ease, speed stretch.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/glyph-slot-roll`, batch 3.3): Char windows 1.2 em wide × 1.7 em tall (34×48 px at 28 px type, 6 px gap), row pitch 1.43 em. Reel cycle: word A glyph, 4 seeded random A–Z, word B glyph, 4 random. Roll 1 at 0.3 s: 5 rows A → B, reel i lasts 0.75 + 0.1·i s (stops 100 ms apart), ease back.out(0.5) (overshoot ≈0.04 row ≈ 6 % of the glyph). Moving glyph scaleY = 1 + min(0.5, 0.03·rows/s), scaleX = 1/√scaleY. Roll 2 at 2.4 s B → A, same timing; loop 4.2 s.
- [`tx-slot-shuffle`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-slot-shuffle) **Slot Shuffle** (Typing & Decoding; text; 800 ms; power3.out; partial, medium · origin react-bits). Closest: `text/slot-roll`. Proposal: text/slot-roll + params rows (seeded random glyphs), alternate direction, speed stretch.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/slot-shuffle`, batch 3.4): Each char window (glyph width + 0.16 em × 1.18 em, card, radius 5 px; row pitch 1.18 em) holds a reel of 6 seeded random letters of its class + the target; the reel travels 6 rows in 0.8 s easeOutCubic (labelled power3.out), reels start 60 ms apart (capped 1 s) from 0.35 s; even slots scroll down, odd up; while fast, scaleY = 1 + min(0.45, 0.035 × rows/s), scaleX = 1/√scaleY. an accent underline appears as a reel lands. Exit fade 3.0–3.3 s; loop 3.4 s.

### text/typewriter: EXTEND · high · effect · ae expression · 3 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: typed.js (1), other:motion.dev (1).

- [`tx-typewriter-loop`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-typewriter-loop) **Type & Backspace Loop** (Typing & Decoding; text; 60 ms; steps(1); partial, high · origin typed.js). Closest: `text/typewriter`, `text/caret-swap`, `ui/typed-prompt`. Proposal: text/typewriter + params words list, erase (backspace speed), backDelay, loop.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/type-backspace-loop`, batch 3.4): Type word 1 at 60 ms / char (demo capped 0.9 s per word), hold (backDelay ≥ 0.7 s; GSAP recipe 1.5 s; demo fills the loop, up to 1.6 s), backspace at 30 ms / char (half the type interval), 0.25–0.3 s pause, type word 2, … (loop or stop on the last). Caret: accent bar ~2.5 px × 1 em, solid while typing / deleting; while idle it blinks as a smooth sine yoyo, 0.5 s per half (ease-in-out; demo floor 0.15 opacity). Demo loop 4.8 s.
- [`tx-human-typewriter`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-human-typewriter) **Humanised Typewriter** (Typing & Decoding; text; 85 ms; steps(1); partial, medium · origin other:motion.dev). Closest: `text/typewriter`, `ui/typed-prompt`, `code/code-terminal-run`. Proposal: text/typewriter + param cadence human (reuse the seeded cadence of ui/typed-prompt).
  Spec (verified; import as `text/human-typewriter`, batch 3.4): Each keystroke interval = 85 ms × (1 ± 0.5 seeded jitter); +320 ms after , . ! ? … and 、。; +120 ms before words of 6+ chars (Latin); caret (accent bar ~2.5 px × 1 em) solid while typing, blinks 0.5 s on / 0.5 s off when idle; optional normalise to a target total duration (demo scales the cadence to fit 2.6 s, from 0.3 s). Demo clears at 3.75 s; loop 4 s.
- [`tx-yakumono-pause`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-yakumono-pause) **Punctuation-Aware Pause** (Japanese; text; 60 ms; steps (per char); partial, medium). Closest: `text/typewriter`, `ui/typed-prompt`. Proposal: text/typewriter + param pauses (punctuation).
  Spec (verified; import as `text/punctuation-pause-type`, batch 3.5): From 0.35 s, chars appear at 60 ms per char (recipe 40–60); +200 ms after '、 , ，' and +400 ms after '。 . ！ ! ？ ?' (recipe 100–200 / 250–400); closing quotes and brackets appear with the previous char and the pause comes after them; schedule compressed to 1.9 s if longer. Each char fades in over 60 ms rising 3 px. Accent caret 2.5 px after the last char: steady while typing, then blinks (0.5 s period, 55 % on). Exit 3.1–3.4 s. Loop 3.6 s.

### transition/crossfade: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 3 motion(s)

- [`tr-dissolve`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tr-dissolve) **Crossfade / Dissolve** (Transitions; dom; 500 ms; linear; partial, high). Closest: `transition/showcase-dissolve`, `transition/fade-through`. Proposal: transition/crossfade (new) transition_in preset.
  Spec (verified; import as `transition/crossfade`, batch 3.1): Incoming scene opacity 0 -> 1 over the outgoing one in 500 ms (atlas: ~12 frames at 24 fps), linear (the demo eases it in-out); the outgoing scene stays at 1 underneath (no dip to the film background) and hides at the end. HF showcase-dissolve ships Crossfade / Color Dip / Blur Crossfade / Focus Pull (Apache-2.0).
- [`tr-blur`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tr-blur) **Blur Crossfade** (Transitions; dom; 600 ms; easeInOutSine; partial, high). Closest: `transition/showcase-dissolve`, `transition/showcase-blur`. Proposal: transition/crossfade (new), param blur (peak 14 px).
  Spec (verified; import as `transition/blur-crossfade`, batch 3.1): Both scenes blur 0 -> 14 px -> 0 as a half-sine over 600 ms (demo peak 10 px); incoming opacity 0 -> 1 easeInOutSine over the same 600 ms, so the swap reads at 300 ms when the blur peaks.
- [`view-crossfade`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=view-crossfade) **View Crossfade** (Layout & Navigation; canvas; 520 ms; easeInOutCubic; partial, high). Closest: `transition/fade-through`. Proposal: transition/crossfade (new) effect usable as transition_in.
  Spec (verified; import as `transition/takayu-crossfade`, batch 3.2): Two scenes overlap: outgoing opacity 1 → 0 and incoming 0 → 1 at the same time, 520 ms power2.inOut (easeInOutCubic), no movement, no empty frame between. Targets [out inner, in inner] as in transition/push.

### data/countdown: EXTEND · high · effect · ae keyframes · 2 motion(s)

- [`catalog-game-cinematic-countdown-go`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-cinematic-countdown-go) **Countdown GO!** (Game Cinematics; canvas; 4000 ms; linear; partial, high). Closest: `data/countdown`, `text/land-in`. Proposal: data/countdown + param style=beats.
  Spec (verified; import as `data/countdown-go`, batch 3.2): px @320x200. Beats 800 ms apart from 0.3 s: digits 3, 2, 1 (60 px) each fade in 80 ms and scale 1.5 → 1 in 250 ms outExpo; at 700-800 ms into the beat they shrink to 0.85 and fade. An accent arc on a grey ring (r 52, stroke 4) shrinks from full to 0 over each beat, anchored at 12 o'clock. 'GO!' at 2.7 s: 300 ms back.out, full accent ring, burst ring r 52 → 82 (stroke 3.5 → 0.5) fading 500 ms outCubic; 4 pips light per beat. Out 3.55-3.8 s in-cubic.
- [`tx-countdown-zoom`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-countdown-zoom) **Countdown Zoom** (Numbers & Data; text; 1000 ms; back.out → ease-in; partial, high). Closest: `data/countdown`, `hand/circle-pointer`. Proposal: data/countdown + param style zoom (one big number per second).
  Spec (corrected; import as `data/countdown-zoom`, batch 3.2): Per second one number: scale 0.6 → 1 in 0.15 s back.out(1.7) (GSAP recipe back.out(2)) with opacity 0 → 1 in ~0.1 s, then scale 1 → 2.2 over the remaining 0.85 s (demo easeInQuad; CSS cubic-bezier(.55,0,1,.45); labelled power2.in) while opacity 1 → 0 on the same curve; a 2.5 px accent arc on a ring (r 72 px) sweeps 0 → 360° once per number, linear. Demo 3-2-1 from 0.2 s, then a final word (accent) pops 0.6 → 1 in 0.25 s back.out and holds; loop 4.2 s.

### effect/letterbox: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 2 motion(s)

- [`catalog-game-cinematic-letterbox-focus`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-cinematic-letterbox-focus) **Letterbox Focus** (Game Cinematics; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, high). Closest: `camera/push-in`. Proposal: effect/letterbox (new).

- [`tx-letterbox-reveal`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-letterbox-reveal) **Letterbox Reveal** (Titles & Film; text; 800 ms; easeInOut; missing, high). Closest: `transition/iris-reveal`. Proposal: title/letterbox (new).

Title (36 px, centred) fades in 0.85–1.35 s (cubic-bezier .65,0,.35,1), 0.5 s into the open. Title out 2.6–2.9 s; bars close 2.85–3.45 s. Loop 3.6 s.

### text/bar-wipe: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 2 motion(s)

- [`tx-bar-wipe-reveal`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-bar-wipe-reveal) **Bar Wipe Reveal** (Mask & Clip; text; 800 ms; ease-in-out; partial, high). Closest: `lower/mask-reveal`. Proposal: text/bar-wipe (new): the lower/mask-reveal bar + clip on any text line.
  Spec (verified; import as `text/bar-wipe`, batch 3.1): Per line (80 ms apart): accent bar (line height × 1.06, 8 px past each side) scaleX 0 → 1 from the left in 0.4 s power2.inOut, then 1 → 0 toward the right in 0.4 s; the text is clipped to the area left of the bar trailing edge, so it appears as the bar pulls away. Exit mirrors (bar grows over the text, text clipped right of the leading edge). Demo: in from 0.3 s, exit from 2.7 s; loop 4 s.
- [`tx-diagonal-cut-reveal`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-diagonal-cut-reveal) **Diagonal Cut Reveal** (Mask & Clip; text; 700 ms; expo.out; partial, medium). Closest: `text/colorama-wipe`, `lower/mask-reveal`. Proposal: text/bar-wipe (new) + params angle (15 deg), unit word, blade.
  Spec (verified; import as `text/diagonal-cut`, batch 3.1): Per word (120 ms apart): clip polygon with a leading edge leaning 15° (top ahead of bottom by height × tan15) moves from left of the word to past its right edge, 0.7 s expo.out; a 5 px accent blade rides the edge and fades over 0.3 s after. Exit: the edge sweeps on hiding the word (0.5 s expo.in, 80 ms apart). Demo: in from 0.3 s, blade fades 0.6–0.9 s after each word starts, exit from 2.8 s; loop 3.8 s.

### text/kinetic-type-swap: EXTEND · high · effect · ae keyframes · 2 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: react-bits (1), aceternity (1).

- [`tx-rotating-letters-spring`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-rotating-letters-spring) **Rotating Text (Spring)** (Swap; text; 450 ms; spring(300, 25); partial, high · origin react-bits). Closest: `text/kinetic-type-swap`, `text/anime-roll`. Proposal: text/kinetic-type-swap + params by letters, spring ease, pill (background resizes on a spring).
  Spec (verified; import as `text/rotating-letters-pill`, batch 3.3): Word in a rounded accent pill (padding 14 px). Every 2 s: old letters y 0 → −120 % and fade, new letters y +100 % → 0 and fade in, each on a spring (stiffness 300, damping 25, ratio ~0.72), letters 30 ms apart; new word waits until the old leaves (~0.22 s). Pill width / height track the new word size on the same spring. Demo: pill height = text + 0.18 em, radius 9 px, dark text on accent, letters clipped to the pill; swaps at 0.7 s and 2.7 s; loop 4 s.
- [`tx-layout-word-insert`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-layout-word-insert) **Layout-animated Word Swap** (Layout & Reflow; text; 400 ms; spring(300, 30); partial, high · origin aceternity). Closest: `text/kinetic-type-swap`, `text/center-build`. Proposal: text/kinetic-type-swap + param reflow (slot width follows each word).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/word-swap-reflow`, batch 3.2): Sentence 30 px with one slot word (last word or [bracketed]): slot = word + 7 px pad each side, 1.5 px accent outline, radius 6, inside a card (radius 8). Swaps at 0.4 / 2.0 / 3.6 s (recipe every 2–3 s): slot width tracks the new word on a spring k 300, c 30 (ζ≈0.87); following text and card move with it. In the slot (clipped): old word exits up 0.7 × slot height with fade, 0.25 s power2.in; new enters from 0.7 × below with fade, 0.35 s expo.out. Loop 5 s.

### text/split-halves: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 2 motion(s)

Word (56 px) cut at its middle: the top half slides in from the left, the bottom half from the right, each from 300 px away (~94 % of the frame width), 450 ms outExpo (0.4 s), then an opposite nudge 3 e^(−10t) cos(30t) px from 0.9 s. Slash line (accent 2.5 px) draws along the seam x 30 → 290 in 200 ms inOutCubic (0.85 s), its tail follows in 350 ms outCubic. 'K.O.' fades in 1.1-1.4 s. Keep room for Vietnamese marks. Demo: split at glyph mid-height (baseline − 0.36 em), lower half tinted accent until 1.0–1.4 s; in at 0.3 s, out at 2.8 s; loop 3.8 s.

### transition/letter-zoom: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 2 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: codrops (1).

- [`tr-letter`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tr-letter) **Mask Wipe through Letterforms** (Transitions; dom; 900 ms; easeInExpo; partial, high). Closest: `text/svg-mask-reveal`, `transition/type-match-cut`. Proposal: transition/letter-zoom (new).
  Spec (verified; import as `transition/letter-zoom`, batch 3.1): Incoming scene shown only inside one huge heavy letter (or short word) centred on the frame: 0-400 ms the letter mask appears, 400-900 ms the mask scales 1 -> 30 easeInExpo until a stroke covers the frame, then the mask is removed. Align the letter's centre with the next subject. In AE: text layer as track matte.
- [`tx-text-window-zoom`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-text-window-zoom) **Text Window Zoom** (Scroll; text; 4200 ms; none (scrub); partial, high · origin codrops). Closest: `text/svg-mask-reveal`. Proposal: transition/letter-zoom (new).
  Spec (corrected; import as `transition/zoom-through-letter`, batch 3.2): Word (92 px, weight 900, up to 2 lines) filled with the next picture through a text mask; a 35 % thin outline shows at rest. Zoom z 0 → 1 over 0.5–2.2 s, cubic-bezier(.65,0,.35,1); scale = 60^z (exponential). Origin = the thickest ink point of the middle letter, and that point slides to the frame centre as z goes 0 → 1. The full picture fades in 1.95–2.3 s linear, leaving only the picture. Loop: jump back at 3.25 s, picture fades out 3.3–3.9 s.

Camera per phrase: centred on it, rotation −its angle, zoom min(220 / w, 70 / h, 3). Phrase i lands at 0.3 + 0.85 i s; the camera moves there in the 0.4 s before, cubic-bezier(.8,0,.2,1), zoom in log space; on landing it pops 0.5 → 1.08 → 1 (0 / 0.12 / 0.24 s, power1.out). ### transition/blinds: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`tr-blinds`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tr-blinds) **Slats / Blinds** (Transitions; dom; 700 ms; easeOutCubic; partial, high). Closest: `transition/showcase-cover`. Proposal: transition/blinds (new).
  Spec (verified; import as `transition/blinds`, batch 3.1): 7 vertical slats of equal width; slat i (left to right) starts at i x 50 ms and opens its width 0 -> full from its left edge over 400 ms easeOutCubic, revealing the incoming scene; ~700 ms total. HF showcase-cover ships horizontal and vertical blinds (Apache-2.0).

### transition/blocks: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`tr-blocks`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tr-blocks) **Staggered Blocks** (Transitions; dom; 800 ms; easeInOutCubic; partial, high). Closest: `transition/showcase-cover`, `transition/grid-pixelate`. Proposal: transition/blocks (new).
  Spec (corrected; import as `transition/block-cover`, batch 3.1): 8 x 5 grid of solid accent tiles (each 1/8 x 1/5 of the frame, 1 px overlap). Cover 0-400 ms: tile (col i, row j) starts (i + j) x 10 ms (demo; the recipe says '30 ms stagger'), scale 0 -> 1 about its centre over 250 ms easeOutCubic, frame covered by 360 ms; cut at 400 ms; clear 400-800 ms in the same diagonal order, scale 1 -> 0 easeInCubic.

### transition/card-flip: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`tr-flip`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tr-flip) **3D Card Flip** (Transitions; dom; 600 ms; easeInOutCubic; partial, high). Closest: `transition/showcase-3d`. Proposal: transition/card-flip (new).
  Spec (verified; import as `transition/card-flip`, batch 3.1): Outgoing scene rotateY 0 -> 90 deg (perspective origin on the hinge, slight 0.9 scale during the turn), at 300 ms hide it and show the incoming from -90 -> 0 deg; 600 ms easeInOutCubic overall; never show a back face (fold to 90 and swap). ### transition/cover-wipe: NEW · high · effect · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)
