### ui/lucky-wheel: NEW · high · block · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`catalog-game-cinematic-lucky-roulette`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-cinematic-lucky-roulette) **Lucky Roulette** (Game Cinematics; canvas; 3800 ms; linear; missing, high). Closest: none. Proposal: ui/lucky-wheel (new).
  Spec (corrected; import as `ui/lucky-wheel-spin`, batch 3.2): px @320x200. Wheel r 60, 6 segments of 60° (alternating greys), labels on radius 40. Spins 3 turns + 100° over 2.05 s (0.3-2.35 s) outQuart. Label opacity 1 − 0.8·clamp(s − 1), with s = 4·(1 − p)^0.75 (p = eased progress): 20 % at full speed. Top pointer tilts back 0.5 rad × min(1, s) × e^(−6f) (f = share of a segment past the last divider). Winning segment fills accent at 2.4 s (150 ms); result 0.8 → 1 (250 ms back.out, 2.45 s). Rewind 3.4-3.75 s.

### ui/hud-bar: NEW · medium · block · ae keyframes · 7 motion(s)

- [`catalog-game-hud-hp-ghost-bar`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-hud-hp-ghost-bar) **HP Ghost Bar** (Game HUD; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `data/chart-story`. Proposal: ui/hud-bar (new) mode=ghost.
  Spec (corrected; import as `ui/hp-ghost-bar`, batch 3.7): px @320x200. Bar 220 x 14, r 4. At the hit (0.6 s) the accent fill jumps 100 → 58 % at once; a light-ink ghost fill behind it (opacity 1 for 100 ms, then 0.7) waits 400 ms then shrinks to 58 % in 500 ms inOut. Bar shakes ±3 px x for 200 ms (per-frame hash, linear decay). Readout 'NN / 100' turns accent; loss label '−42' centred in the gap while the gap > 3 %. Heal back 400 ms inOut at 2.3 s. Skip the time graph (lesson).
- [`catalog-game-hud-stamina-pulse`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-hud-stamina-pulse) **Stamina Pulse** (Game HUD; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `data/chart-story`. Proposal: ui/hud-bar (new) mode=threshold-pulse.
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/stamina-low-pulse`, batch 3.7): px @320x200. Bar 220 x 14, r 4. Value steps 80 → 52 % (0.3-0.42 s) and 52 → 22 % (0.75-0.87 s), each 120 ms outCubic; dashed tick at 30 %. Below it the fill turns accent (from green) and pulses: opacity 1 → 0.5 and an outline 3 px outside (r 6, stroke 1.5) at 0 → 80 %, cosine period 800 ms, envelope in 200 ms from 0.9 s. Recovery to 80 % (2.1-2.6 s outCubic) ends the pulse 2.12-2.3 s. '−28' / '−30' float up 14 px over 700 ms, fade 350-700 ms.
- [`catalog-game-hud-resource-cap`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-hud-resource-cap) **Resource Cap** (Game HUD; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `data/chart-story`. Proposal: ui/hud-bar (new) mode=cap.
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/resource-cap-bar`, batch 3.7): px @320x200. Bar 220 x 16 (r 4) fills 35 → 100 % linear over 1.0 s (0.3-1.3 s); MAX tick at the end. '+50' chips every 250 ms from 0.3 s fall 14 px in 200 ms in-quad at the fill edge, fade 180-260 ms. Chips after the cap fall 6 px, bounce up 7 px (300 ms), turn grey, get struck through, fade 300-550 ms. At full the bar turns accent; 'FULL' pill scales 0.9 → 1 in 120 ms and blinks (cosine, 240 ms period) until 1.78 s, then holds. Reset 2.3-2.7 s inOut.
- [`catalog-game-combat-combo-counter`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-combat-combo-counter) **Combo Counter** (Combat & Hit FX; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `text/land-in`, `data/count-up`. Proposal: ui/hud-bar (new) mode=grace.
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/combo-counter`, batch 3.7): px @320x200. Hits at 0.35, 0.70, 1.05, 1.40 s: number (46 px, accent) steps +1 with scale 1.4 → 1 in 250 ms outCubic; grace bar 80 x 6 refills in 60 ms outCubic (light ink for 100 ms, then accent) and drains linearly over 900 ms. When empty (2.36 s) the combo breaks over 340 ms: number greys, drops 10 px, shrinks 15 %, fades; 'broken' label in. Per hit: hitter flash 120 ms, squash 12 % (scaleX 1.12 / Y 0.88, 250 ms half-sine), 7-dot burst to 18 px.
- [`catalog-game-progress-energy-refill`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-progress-energy-refill) **Energy Refill** (Progression & Quests; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `data/chart-story`, `data/count-up`. Proposal: ui/hud-bar (new) mode=charge.
  Spec (corrected; import as `ui/battery-refill`, batch 3.6): px @320x200. Battery outline 170 x 52 (r 9, 2 px) + tip, 5 segments (dark dividers). Fill 15 → 100 % inOut over 1.45 s (0.45-1.9 s); its opacity pulses 1 → 0.6 at 2.5 Hz whenever not full; the percent readout counts. Bolt glyph grey, switching to the dark bg colour once the fill passes it (52 %). Full 1.9-2.5 s: pulse stops, battery pops 5 % (200 ms half-sine), readout accent, 'FULL' pops at 1.95 s (200 ms back.out). Drain to 15 % 2.5-2.72 s in-cubic.
- [`catalog-game-progress-xp-overflow`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-progress-xp-overflow) **XP Overflow** (Progression & Quests; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `data/chart-story`, `text/anime-roll`. Proposal: ui/hud-bar (new) mode=overflow.
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/xp-overflow-bar`, batch 3.6): px @320x200. Bar 220 x 14 (r 7). '+45 XP' fades in rising 6 px (0.2-0.45 s outCubic), out 0.7-0.9 s. Fill 80 → 100 % (500 ms outCubic, 0.3 s); light-ink flash over the bar 70 % → 0 (0.8-1.2 s); level rolls 07 → 08 (36 px, clipped, 250 ms back.out at 0.85 s, bump 15 % 0.9-1.2 s). The bar jumps to 0 at 1.05 s and refills to 25 % (400 ms outCubic at 1.2 s); '+25' bracket fades in 1.5-1.7 s; 3 chips light at 0.8, 1.0, 1.4 s. Back to 80 % 2.4-2.65 s inOut.
- [`catalog-game-impact-fx-combo-heat`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-impact-fx-combo-heat) **Combo Heat Escalation** (Game Impact FX; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `motion/spring-track`, `data/count-up`. Proposal: ui/hud-bar (new) mode=heat.
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/combo-heat-gauge`, batch 3.1): px @320x200. Hits at 0.35, 0.8, 1.15, 1.45, 1.7, 1.9 s: count 1 → 6 (56 px + 'HIT') with pop 1 + 0.35 e^(−12t) cos(20t); colour stages blue (≤ 2), yellow (≤ 4), accent; gauge 200 x 12 (dividers at 1/3, 2/3) follows n/6 by a sum of springs k260 d22, new colour cross-fading in 150 ms; COOL / WARM / HOT labels light. From hit 5, 10 sparks loop up 30 px above the gauge's right third (0.9 Hz). Reset: gauge → 0 at 3.4 s, number out 3.4-3.8 s.

### text/char-loop: NEW · medium · effect · ae keyframes · 6 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: other:splitting.js (1).

- [`tx-zero-gravity-drift`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-zero-gravity-drift) **Zero-gravity Drift** (Glyph Physics; text; 4500 ms; sine.inOut; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/float-list`, `house/deco-float`. Proposal: text/char-loop (new), mode drift.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/zero-gravity`, batch 3.3): Per char (letter spacing 0.1 em): x and y offset 12 px × sin(2πt / P + φ), rotation 15° × sin(2πt / P + φ), with separate seeded periods P in 3–6 s and seeded phases φ for x, y and rotation. Amount ramps 0 → 1 over 0.3–1.5 s (cubic-bezier .65,0,.35,1) and the whole line lifts 6 px with it; ramps back 4.6–5.8 s. Loop 6 s.
- [`tx-wave-loop`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-wave-loop) **Wave Loop** (Loops & Decoration; text; 1200 ms; ease-in-out; partial, medium · origin other:splitting.js). Closest: `house/bob`, `motion/float-list`. Proposal: text/char-loop (new), mode wave.
  Spec (verified; import as `text/wave-loop`, batch 3.5): Per char: y = −0.3 em × (0.5 − 0.5 cos(2π (t − 0.1 i) / 1.2)) (rise 0 → −0.3 em → 0 over 1.2 s, sine.inOut), delay 100 ms × i (≈0.5 rad per char; recipe 60–100 ms), repeating forever, so the crest travels left → right. Optional: chars near the crest (lift > 60 %) tint accent. Seamless loop 2.4 s. AE: amount 50 + 50 × sin(2πft + index × 0.5).
- [`tx-scale-breathe`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-scale-breathe) **Wiggly Scale Breathe** (Loops & Decoration; text; 1600 ms; sine; partial, medium). Closest: `house/breathe`. Proposal: text/char-loop (new), mode breathe.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/scale-breathe`, batch 3.5): Per char (letter spacing 0.08 em): uniform scale 1 + 0.12 × sin(2π f t + φ), f seeded as 0.5 or 0.75 Hz (period 2 s or 1.33 s), φ seeded phase; origin about 0.15 em below the glyph centre. Chars above 1.07 tint toward accent (up to 80 %). Seamless loop 4 s.
- [`tx-jump-sequence`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-jump-sequence) **Sequential Hop** (Loops & Decoration; text; 400 ms; ease-out / ease-in (parabola); partial, medium). Closest: `house/hop`. Proposal: text/char-loop (new), mode hop.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/hop-sequence`, batch 3.5): Cycle 2.4 s. Char i hops at 0.2 s + 0.1 s × i (capped at 1.5 s total): 0.1 s crouch before (sx 1.05, sy 0.93 at peak), then a 0.4 s parabolic hop y = −0.4 em × 4u(1 − u) (power1.out up, power1.in down), then a 0.12 s landing squash peaking sx 1.1 / sy 0.86; origin at the baseline. The hopping char is accent; a floor shadow shrinks and fades with height.
- [`tx-y-spin-stagger`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-y-spin-stagger) **Staggered Y Spin** (Loops & Decoration; text; 800 ms; power2.inOut; partial, medium). Closest: `text/land-in`. Proposal: text/char-loop (new), mode spin.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/y-spin-stagger`, batch 3.5): Per char rotateY 0 → 360° in 0.8 s from 0.3 s + 0.08 s × i (capped at 1 s total), demo ease power1.inOut (recipe power2.inOut). Drawn as scaleX = cos θ (no perspective) with scaleY 1 + 0.05 × /sin θ/; back side (cos < 0) accent. Cycle 3.2 s (≈1.5 s rest).
- [`tx-loading-dots`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-loading-dots) **Loading Dots** (Loops & Decoration; text; 1200 ms; ease-in-out; partial, low). Closest: `ui/typing-indicator`. Proposal: text/char-loop (new), mode dots.
  Spec (verified; import as `text/loading-dots`, batch 3.5): Word still (trailing dots in the text removed). Three round dots (radius 0.09 em, 0.3 em apart, starting 0.7 gap after the last char, middle one accent). Dot k: v = sin(π ph / 0.6) for ph < 0.6 of a 1.2 s cycle, else 0, with delay 0.2 k s; opacity 0.2 + 0.8 v, lift v × 0.22 em (recipe −25 %). Seamless loop 2.4 s.

### text/text-on-path: NEW · medium · effect · ae expression · 6 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: magicui (1), react-bits (1).

- [`tx-circular-text-spin`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-circular-text-spin) **Circular Text Spin** (Path & Shape; text; 10000 ms; linear; missing, medium · origin magicui). Closest: none. Proposal: text/text-on-path (new), mode circle.
  Spec (verified; import as `text/circle-spin`, batch 3.6): Text + ' • ' (Japanese '・', separator accent) repeated m times so it fills the circle (demo radius 58 px, chars ≤ 24 px); char placed by arc length, rotated to the tangent with tops outward. The ring turns 360° per 10–20 s linear, seamless. Optional speed-up ×4 eased in 1.5–1.9 s and out 3.1–3.5 s (cubic-bezier .65,0,.35,1), rotation = integral of speed. Demo loop 5 s.
- [`tx-curved-loop-marquee`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-curved-loop-marquee) **Curved Loop Marquee** (Path & Shape; text; 4000 ms; linear; missing, medium · origin react-bits). Closest: `title/perspective-marquee`. Proposal: text/text-on-path (new), mode loop.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/curved-marquee`, batch 3.6): Text + ' • ' (separator accent) repeated along an open curve; demo curve spans the frame plus 30 px each side: y = 30 px × sin(2π × 0.9u − 0.5) (one wave and a bit), chars 24 px placed by arc length and rotated to the tangent (chord ±3 px). Offset moves linearly about 110 px/s (1.8 px per frame at 60 fps; recipe 2 px/frame), wrapping every whole unit so there is no seam. Chars fade over 40 px at both frame edges. Loop 4 s.
- [`tx-text-along-path`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-text-along-path) **Text Along Path** (Path & Shape; text; 1200 ms; easeOutCubic → easeInCubic; missing, medium). Closest: `hand/path-text`, `motion/anime-path`. Proposal: text/text-on-path (new), mode run.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/path-run`, batch 3.6): Text on an open path (demo: one full sine wave, amplitude 40 px, across the frame plus 50 px each side); each glyph at its arc-length point, rotated to the tangent (chord ±6 px). Text centre runs from fully off the start (−half width − 20 px) to the path middle 0.3–1.5 s power2.out, holds 1.1 s, then to fully off the end 2.6–3.7 s power2.in. Size fits about 190 px of text (12–34 px). Last char accent. Loop 4 s.
- [`tx-spiral-text`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-spiral-text) **Spiral Text** (Path & Shape; text; 8000 ms; linear; missing, low). Closest: none. Proposal: text/text-on-path (new), mode spiral.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/spiral-flow`, batch 3.6): Text + ' • ' (separator accent) repeated on an Archimedean spiral of 3.2 turns, r = 72 px × θ / θmax. Font size = 0.24 × r, so each char takes a constant angle (0.24 × its width in em). The string flows inward linearly about 1.8 rad of spiral angle per 5 s (whole units, seamless). Each glyph rotated to its angle + 90°; fades in from the outer end over 1.4 rad and out near the centre (r 6 → 28 px). Demo loop 5 s (recipe: 8 s loop).
- [`tx-morphing-path-ride`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-morphing-path-ride) **Morphing Path Ride** (Path & Shape; text; 1500 ms; ease-in-out; missing, low). Closest: `shape/anime-morph`. Proposal: text/text-on-path (new) + param morph (the rail changes shape).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/path-morph-ride`, batch 3.6): Text centred by arc length on a rail (size fits ~200 px). Shapes, same point count: line 240 px; wave = same line with y − 20 px × sin(2πu); arch = arc of radius 124 px from angle π + 0.62 to 2π − 0.62 (200 px wide, ends level with the line, top 54 px up). Morph line → wave 0.4–1.9 s, wave → arch 2.6–4.1 s, arch → line 4.9–6.2 s, each cubic-bezier(.65,0,.35,1), point-by-point lerp; glyphs re-laid on the path every frame. Loop 6.4 s.
- [`tx-shape-contour-text`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-shape-contour-text) **Shape Contour Text** (Path & Shape; text; 10000 ms; linear; missing, low). Closest: none. Proposal: text/text-on-path (new), mode contour.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/shape-contour`, batch 3.6): Closed shape (demo heart 122 px tall, filled card colour with a 1 px outline). Text + ' • ' (separator accent) repeated m times so it exactly fills the perimeter, with size ≤ 18 px. Glyph baselines follow the outline, angle taken from a chord of ±1.1 em so cusps stay smooth. Offset moves forward linearly one repeat per 5 s (recipe: string × 2, startOffset 0 → −50 % per 10 s), seamless. Loop 5 s.

- [`catalog-game-progress-rank-up`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-progress-rank-up) **Rank Up** (Progression & Quests; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `text/anime-roll`, `house/logo-pop`. Proposal: effect/spark-burst (new) + clip fill-up.
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/rank-up-badge`, batch 3.6): px @320x200. Shield badge (×1.25, grey). Wind-up 1 → 0.92 (280 ms inOut, 0.7 s), then pops from 0.92 to 1 by spring k300 d11 at 1.0 s. Accent fills bottom → top through a clip in 220 ms outCubic; the letter rolls B → A (40 px) in the same 220 ms; the second chevron lights past halfway. 14 four-point sparks (accent, every 3rd yellow) fly from radius 30 to 70-100 px over 700 ms outCubic, size 5.5 → 1, fading. - [`catalog-game-impact-fx-parry-time-slice`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-impact-fx-parry-time-slice) **Parry Time Slice** (Game Impact FX; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `hand/hit-stop`. Proposal: effect/spark-burst (new) mode=gravity.

Then they deflect to −0.62 rad in 450 ms outExpo with wobble 0.08 e^(−6t) sin(30t) rad; the star shrinks in 350 ms. 9 sparks fan upward ±73° (±6° seeded) at 90-150 px/s, y += 200·t² (400 px/s²), 6 px streaks, fading over 600 ms.

Burst at 0.65 s: 20 dots alternating the 2 colours fly to 62-88 px (vertical × 0.82) over 900 ms outCubic, size 4.5 → 2.5, with fading streaks behind; dots stay until 3.5 s; ring r 10 → 50 (stroke 3) in 400 ms outCubic; centre star 1.6 → 1 spring k300 d14. Out 3.5-3.9 s in-cubic.

### house/glow-card: EXTEND · medium · effect · ae png · 5 motion(s)

- [`glow-pulse`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=glow-pulse) **Glow Pulse** (Light & Glow; dom; 1100 ms; easeInOutSine; partial, medium). Closest: `house/glow-card`, `house/twinkle`. Proposal: house/glow-card + param pulse.
  Spec (corrected; import as `effect/glow-pulse`, batch 3.2): Looping glow on a 36 px accent dot, 1100 ms per cycle, each half eased in-out (site runtime bezier .45,.05,.2,1; recipe sine.inOut): box-shadow blur 8 → 26 → 8 px (recipe 8 → 24, opacity 35 → 85 %), brightness 0.92 → 1.22, scale 0.98 → 1.03 (the target is not still). Behind it a 48 px ring with an 8 px accent border: scale 0.9 → 1.35, opacity 0.28 → 0.9, blur 5 → 10 px, same cycle.
- [`rim-glow`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=rim-glow) **Rim Glow** (Light & Glow; canvas; 1000 ms; easeInOutSine; partial, medium). Closest: `house/glow-card`, `lower/neon-border`. Proposal: house/glow-card + param pulse + rim.
  Spec (corrected; import as `effect/rim-glow`, batch 3.2): Looping rim light on a dark silhouette (r 50 disc): stroke 2 → 4 → 2 px and outer glow 10 → 22 → 10 (drawn as a shadow blur of 0.8× that: 8 → 17.6 px), 1000 ms per cycle (sine ping-pong, 500 ms each way); a 1 px outer ring at radius + stroke and up to 50 % opacity follows the peak; the inside stays dark.
- [`aurora-glow`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=aurora-glow) **Aurora Glow** (Light & Glow; canvas; 1500 ms; easeInOutSine; partial, medium). Closest: `background/aurora-drift`, `house/glow-card`. Proposal: house/glow-card + param orbit.
  Spec (verified; import as `effect/aurora-glow`, batch 3.2): Behind a card: two blurred glows (radius ~41 px, blur 14 px, additive, opacity 0.55 / 0.5) circle opposite each other at offset (±24 px x, ±18 px y) along an angle swinging −1.3 → +1.3 rad, 1500 ms sine ping-pong; colours shift up to 70° in hue (red→blue, blue→green). The card on top stays still.
- [`catalog-ui-button-pulse-glow`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-button-pulse-glow) **Pulse Glow** (UI Buttons; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `house/glow-card`. Proposal: house/glow-card + param pulse (glow breathes 0 to 1 to 0 on a loop, with outward halo rings).
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/pulse-glow`, batch 3.4): Button 140x44, radius 14, scale fixed at 1. Glow I goes 0 → 1 → 0 on a sine over 2.8 s: border width 1.5 + I px (border opacity 0.25 + 0.75 I), accent shadow blur 18 × I, label opacity 0.55 + 0.45 × I. Two halo outlines (1.5 px) start at 1.0 s and 1.35 s, grow 3 → 27 px out from the edge over 1.2 s power2.out, opacity 0.7 → 0. Loops.
- [`catalog-ui-button-breathing-gradient`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-button-breathing-gradient) **Breathing Gradient** (UI Buttons; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `house/glow-card`, `background/mesh-gradient`. Proposal: house/glow-card + param breathe (seamless glow loop with a drifting gradient centre).
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/breathing-gradient`, batch 3.4): Button 140x46, radius 16, seamless 2.8 s loop: glow I = 0.5 − 0.5 cos(2π t / 2.8); radial gradient (yellow 0 → accent 0.55 → blue 1, radius 90 px) centred at x = centre + 40 px × sin(2π t / 2.8); shadow blur 4 + 14 I; outer halo ring (1.2 px) offset 6 + 4 I px, opacity 0.15 + 0.35 I. Frame 0 equals frame 2.8 s.

### text/rgb-glitch: EXTEND · medium · effect · ae keyframes · 5 motion(s)

Keys over 900 ms easeInOutCubic: 0 % +-34, 42 % +-30 (slow hold), 68 % +-12 (opacity 0.95), 90 % 0 (opacity 0), hold to 100 %. Core on keys 0/20/38/56/100 %: scale 0.82 / 1.08 / 0.98 / 1.02 / 1, x 0 / +4 / -3 / +1 / 0 px; opacity 0.35 -> 1 and blur 2 -> 0 px by 20 %. Colours cyan #00e0ff, red #ff3060.
- [`lk-riso`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=lk-riso) **Misregistration** (Texture & Look; dom; 125 ms; steps(3); partial, medium). Closest: `text/rgb-glitch`, `title/prism`, `hand/boil`. Proposal: text/rgb-glitch + param mode riso.
  Spec (verified; import as `effect/riso-misregister`, batch 3.6): Two copies of the art in print inks (cyan #2ec4e8, pink #ff4fa3) blended multiply, offset in opposite directions by (o, o/2): o steps 4 -> 3 -> 2 -> 1 -> 2 -> 3 px, one step per 12 fps frame (~83 ms; recipe 4 -> 3 -> 2 over 3 frames), cycling.
- [`catalog-ui-material-chromatic-glass-edge`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-material-chromatic-glass-edge) **Chromatic Glass Edge** (Material & Surface Motion; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `text/rgb-glitch`. Proposal: text/rgb-glitch + mode speed (outline split proportional to speed).
  Spec (verified; import as `effect/speed-chroma-edge`, batch 3.5): Card 112x80 r14 (60 % opaque) moves x 104 → 216 (0.4-1.3 s power2.inOut) and back (2.3-3.2 s). Two outline copies (yellow, blue, 2 px, additive) shift by −/+ offset where offset = 6 px × clamp(speed / 230 px/s) in the travel direction; the normal accent outline fades out as the offset passes 3 px. 0 px at rest. Background vertical bars show through.
- [`tx-rgb-split-glitch`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-rgb-split-glitch) **RGB Split Glitch** (Distortion & Glitch; text; 160 ms; steps; partial, medium · origin react-bits). Closest: `text/rgb-glitch`, `text/scan-band`. Proposal: text/rgb-glitch + param slices (copies clipped to seeded horizontal bands per burst).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/rgb-split-glitch`, batch 3.6): Two copies over the text, each clipped to a horizontal band. CSS: cyan copy (x −2 px, cyan shadow +5 px) on a 2 s cycle, red copy (x +2, red shadow −5 px) on 3 s; bands slide through 6 inset keys (20/50, 60/12, 43/40, 8/78, 70/6, 32/55 %), linear alternate-reverse. Canvas demo (12 fps): bursts only in 0.35–1.05 / 2.4–2.78 s on ~70 % of frames: red band in the upper half, cyan in the lower (6–22 % tall), shifted −/+4–12 px, screen blend; text jitters ±2 px. Loop 3 s.
- [`tx-slice-glitch`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-slice-glitch) **Slice Displacement** (Distortion & Glitch; text; 67 ms; steps(1); partial, medium). Closest: `effect/image-glitch-burst`, `text/rgb-glitch`. Proposal: text/rgb-glitch + param slices (6-8 seeded slices shifting +-24 px, colour optional).
  Spec (verified; import as `text/slice-glitch`, batch 3.6): 6–8 horizontal slices (cut positions jittered ±35 % per burst); in a burst (1–3 frames at 15 fps updates, 50–80 ms) slices with seed > 0.45 shift x by a seeded ±24 px; 18 % ghost of the clean text underneath; bursts only in set windows (e.g. 0.3–1.15 s and 2.25–2.75 s). Demo: 7 slices, steps of 67 ms in groups of 4 (burst if seed > 0.25), the most-shifted slice turns accent; loop 3 s. CSS fallback: 4 band copies, 2.4 s steps, jolt −18 / +11 px at 90 / 92 %.

### hand/boil: EXTEND · medium · effect · ae keyframes · 4 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: aceternity (1).

- [`tx-wiggly-jitter`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-wiggly-jitter) **Wiggly Jitter** (Emphasis; text; 125 ms; noise (per frame); partial, medium). Closest: `hand/boil`, `motion/chaos-shake`. Proposal: hand/boil + param split chars (independent noise per char).
  Spec (verified; import as `text/wiggly-jitter`, batch 3.3): Split chars; each char has its own seeded noise: x, y ±6 px, rotate ±5°, ~8 changes / s (smooth noise or 3 steps per 0.24 s), correlation 0; amplitude eases 0 → 1 over 0.3 s (outQuad) and back to 0 over 0.3 s at the end (inQuad). Demo: in 0.3–0.6 s, out 2.5–2.8 s, loop 3 s (a second, 100 % correlated copy shows the whole word shaking as one).
- [`tx-turbulence-wobble`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-turbulence-wobble) **Turbulence Wobble** (Distortion & Glitch; text; 100 ms; steps; partial, low · origin aceternity). Closest: `hand/boil`, `hand/boil-hf`. Proposal: hand/boil + param displace (SVG turbulence displacement, seed stepped 10x / s).
  Spec (verified; import as `text/turbulence-wobble`, batch 3.6): SVG filter on the text: feTurbulence baseFrequency 0.02, numOctaves 2, seed = floor(t × 10) (new pose every 100 ms; the SMIL source cycles seeds 0–9 each second); feDisplacementMap scale 5–15 px (SVG 10), so outlines squirm like boiling line art. GSAP: scale 0 → 12, 0.6 s sine.inOut yoyo, 0.8 s rest. Demo: scale ramps 0 → 12 over 0.3–0.8 s, back 2.4–2.85 s; loop 3 s.
- [`tx-film-scratch-jitter`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-film-scratch-jitter) **Scratched Film Jitter** (Titles & Film; text; 42 ms; steps(1); partial, medium). Closest: `hand/boil`, `effect/film-grain`. Proposal: hand/boil + params flicker (opacity 0.85–1) and ghost (offset double exposure).
  Spec (corrected; import as `hand/film-jitter`, batch 3.2): 24 fps, seeded per frame. Visible 0.3–0.38, 0.5–0.62, 0.75–0.8, then 0.92–2.8 s. Block jitter ±2 px; per letter ±1.5 px and ±1.7°; opacity flicker 0.85–1 with a matching full-frame darkening (0–15 %). Double exposure: mute copy at 35 %, offset +2–5 px x and half that up. Thin dark scratches inside the letters; 0–2 vertical scratches, ~50 dust specks and an occasional blob per frame; optional sprocket edges shifting 3 px every other frame. Loop 3.2 s.
- [`tx-telop-shake`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-telop-shake) **Shaking Telop** (Broadcast & Telop; text; 100 ms; random; partial, medium). Closest: `hand/boil`. Proposal: hand/boil + param split: chars.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/telop-shiver`, batch 3.5): Per char, updated on a 24 fps grid: x, y offset (±3 px, recipe 2–6) and rotation (±3°) from smooth value noise running at 10 units / s; neighbours' noise phases differ by 0.7 × 3.7 units (≈30 % correlation). Telop style: heavy text (spacing 0.03 em), ink fill, second-accent outer stroke 0.3 em, dark offset (2, 2) px shadow. Fades in 0.3–0.42 s, off at 2.7 s. Loop 3 s.

### motion/drag-release: EXTEND · medium · effect · ae keyframes · 4 motion(s)

- [`rubber-band`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=rubber-band) **Rubber Band** (Bounce & Reaction; canvas; 900 ms; easeOutElastic; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/drag-release`, `transition/rubber-band`. Proposal: motion/drag-release + param stretch.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/rubber-stretch`, batch 3.5): A cursor grabs a pill and pulls it x +105 px with scaleX 1 → 1.8 (scaleY = 1/scaleX) over 380 ms power2.inOut; on release x and scaleX return by one spring (k 300, d 11): scaleX dips to ~0.72 then 1, x settles at a new rest +37 px from the start, ~520 ms. (Demo px.) The spring overshoots 35 %; the site returns the pill to its first slot in 400 ms at 2000 ms.
- [`snap-back`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=snap-back) **Snap Back** (Bounce & Reaction; canvas; 560 ms; easeOutBack; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/drag-release`. Proposal: motion/drag-release + param stretch.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/snap-back`, batch 3.5): Pulled right: x +98 px (demo) and scaleX 1 → 1.5 (scaleY = 1/scaleX) in 550 ms power2.inOut, a 200 ms hold with a ±0.6 px tremble, then release: x returns on a spring (k 420, d 20), scaleX on a looser one (k 420, d 12) dipping to ~0.8 before 1; 4 ghost copies 18 ms apart for 300 ms after release; a tether line thins as it stretches.
- [`drag-resistance`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=drag-resistance) **Drag Resistance** (Bounce & Reaction; canvas; 820 ms; easeInOutBack; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/drag-release`. Proposal: motion/drag-release + param stretch.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/drag-resistance`, batch 3.5): Grab the right edge: it moves +92 px in 600 ms power2.inOut while the left edge lags (follows 100 ms later, width kept within 80–108 px: scaleX up to 1.35, height 1/scaleX); release: the pill springs back (k 220, d 16) ~12 px past its slot and settles, stretching up to +18 % with speed on the way.
- [`drag-inertia`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=drag-inertia) **Drag Inertia** (Web Interaction; canvas; 1000 ms; easeOutQuart; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/drag-release`, `cursor/swipe-rail`. Proposal: motion/drag-release + param throw (keep the release speed, decay x0.88 per frame, no spring back).
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/drag-inertia`, batch 3.5): Knob 36x36 r8 in a 264x52 pill track (stops at both ends). Cursor grabs 0.35 s (knob scale 1.06 while held); 0.45-0.65 s dragged 60 px right (power2.in); released, it glides x = 117 × (1 − e^(−7.67 t)) (= speed × 0.88 per 60 fps frame), at rest by about 1.85 s. While moving scaleX = 1 + min(0.14, speed / 6000 px/s), scaleY inverse; velocity ticks trail below. Mirror throw back 1.85-3.3 s landing exactly on the start.

### motion/inertial-bounce: EXTEND · medium · effect · ae keyframes · 4 motion(s)

- [`catalog-ui-button-jelly-squash`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-button-jelly-squash) **Jelly Squash** (UI Buttons; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/inertial-bounce`. Proposal: motion/inertial-bounce + param squash (scaleX = 1 + d, scaleY = 1 / (1 + d), origin bottom).
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/jelly-squash`, batch 3.4): On press (0.55 s): d(t) = 0.2 × e^(−3.4 t) × cos(17 t), ramped in over 50 ms (power1.out); scaleX = 1 + d, scaleY = 1 / (1 + d) (area kept), origin bottom centre; the wobble lasts about 1.3 s and is faded to 0 between 1.6-2.0 s after the press. Equivalent spring k290 d6.8 (site value). Button 140x46 r16.
- [`catalog-ui-button-wobble-tilt`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-button-wobble-tilt) **Wobble Tilt** (UI Buttons; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/inertial-bounce`, `motion/pendulum-swing`. Proposal: motion/inertial-bounce + param kick (swing from rest about a bottom pivot).
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/wobble-tilt`, batch 3.4): From rest at the tap (0.5 s): angle = 0.24 × e^(−2.4 t) × sin(9 t) rad (first swing about 9°), pivot at the bottom centre; faded to 0 between 1.5-1.9 s after the tap. Equivalent spring k144 d5.6 (site value). Button 130x44 r14.
- [`ty-inertia`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=ty-inertia) **Inertia Letters** (Glyph Physics; dom; 900 ms; damped sine; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/inertial-bounce`, `text/land-in`. Proposal: motion/inertial-bounce + param split: chars (lanes alternate ±120 px).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/inertia-letters`, batch 3.3): Per letter (demo INERTIA, 36 px): starts at 0.3 + i × 0.08 s, flies 120 px in from below (even i) or above (odd i) in 0.25 s power2.out. After landing it rotates θ = 0.5 rad (28.6°) × e^(−t / 0.25) × sin(30 t) about its baseline centre (period 0.21 s ≈ 12.6 frames at 60 fps; under 3° after 0.6 s). First letter accent. Loop 3 s.
- [`tx-path-inertia`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-path-inertia) **Path With Tracking Inertia** (Path & Shape; text; 900 ms; inertia (damped sine); partial, medium). Closest: `motion/inertial-bounce`, `text/tracking-in`. Proposal: motion/inertial-bounce + prop letterSpacing.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/tracking-inertia`, batch 3.6): Text on a shallow U path (y = 0.0007 × (x − centre)² px). Head runs 340 px to its stop 0.3–1.2 s with ease ½ linear + ½ power1.out (still moving at the stop). From the stop: per-gap tracking = −0.25 em × sin(2π × 3 × t) × e^(−6t), each char offset by that × its index from the end, so the trailing chars squeeze in, overshoot open and settle in ~1 s. Fade out 3.4–3.8 s. Loop 4.2 s. AE: inertia expression amp 0.05, freq 3, decay 6 on Tracking Amount.

Leave 1.86 s: shrinks to 0 at the exit point in 440 ms power2.in. Cursor (power2.inOut): (100,186) to 0.2 s, (122,120) 0.6, (146,106) 0.9, (150,104) 1.7, (252,60) 2.05, (266,176) 2.3, start 2.6.
- [`catalog-ui-button-tap-ripple`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-button-tap-ripple) **Tap Ripple** (UI Buttons; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `cursor/press-ripple`. Proposal: ui/ink-ripple (new).

- [`catalog-ui-button-color-splash`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-button-color-splash) **Color Splash** (UI Buttons; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; missing, medium). Closest: none. Proposal: ui/ink-ripple (new) mode splash (hard-edged state wipe from the contact point).

Tap 2 (1.7 s, right-high point (214,80)) brings the old state (dark card, light label) back the same way. A small dot marks each tap for 0.3 s.

### effect/gooey-split: EXTEND · medium · block · ae png · 3 motion(s)

- [`svg-gooey-merge`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=svg-gooey-merge) **Gooey Merge** (SVG & Path; canvas; 1050 ms; easeInOutCubic; partial, medium). Closest: `effect/gooey-split`, `text/morph-text`. Proposal: shape/gooey (new): goo filter on a group of the film's shapes.
  Spec (verified; import as `shape/gooey-merge`, batch 3.3): Two circles r 26 px in a group with a goo filter (Gaussian blur 7 px, then alpha x 22 - 9). Centres start +-64 px from the middle, hold 300 ms, move to +-22 px over 1000 ms easeInOut (a neck forms before contact), hold 900 ms, return over 900 ms. One fill colour.
- [`catalog-ui-material-gel-button-split`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-material-gel-button-split) **Gel Button Split** (Material & Surface Motion; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `effect/gooey-split`. Proposal: effect/gooey-split + variant pill split in two.
  Spec (verified; import as `effect/gel-split`, batch 3.5): Two pills 60x44 start overlapped (centres ±22). 0.3-0.5 s squeeze to ±18 (power2.out, scaleX 0.94); 0.5-1.3 s pull apart to ±54 (power1.in), scaleX to 1.16, scaleY = 1 / √scaleX, neck width 22 × scaleY × (1 − (d − 22) / 32)^1.4, reaching 0 at 1.3 s (split). Then centres to ±74 on a spring k260 d12 with a scaleX wobble 1 + 0.16 × (1 − spring k300 d9). Rejoin at 3.0 s on a spring k200 d22 (scaleX bump 1.05), neck back after 0.18 s.
- [`catalog-ui-material-bubble-merge`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-material-bubble-merge) **Bubble Merge Menu** (Material & Surface Motion; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `effect/gooey-split`. Proposal: effect/gooey-split + variant merge (two bubbles fuse into one pill).
  Spec (verified; import as `effect/bubble-merge`, batch 3.5): Two circles r24 approach from 88 to 24 px from the centre (0.3-1.1 s power1.in) with 8 % squash. Contact 1.1-1.25 s: a neck grows to full (power2.out) and they close to 19. Then one pill: width 86 → 156, height 48 → 58 on a spring k220 d16; 'A + B' fades in 1.45-1.7 s. Split back: pill shrinks 3.0-3.25 s (power2.in), neck breaks 3.25-3.4 s, circles part to 88 (3.4-4.0 s power2.out).

### hand/shake: EXTEND · medium · effect · ae keyframes · 3 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: other:gsap (1).

A small star flash at the contact for 140 ms. The same mode serves error shakes and bell rings (rotation, pivot top).
- [`e-wiggle`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=e-wiggle) **CustomWiggle** (Easing; dom; 600 ms; CustomWiggle(6, easeOut); partial, medium · origin other:gsap). Closest: `hand/shake`. Proposal: hand/shake + param mode:"wiggle".
  Spec (corrected; import as `motion/ease-wiggle`, batch 3.3): CustomWiggle(6, easeOut): the value swings 6 times around rest, amplitude falling linearly to 0, ending at rest; recipe 600 ms. Site demo: a bell rotates sin(6·2π p)·(1 − p)·0.5 rad (±28.6° at first) about its top over 900 ms. Mode params: prop x / y / rotation, cycles, amp, decay linear / exp; a regular sine, not noise.
- [`catalog-game-impact-fx-camera-kick`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-impact-fx-camera-kick) **Camera Kick HUD** (Game Impact FX; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `hand/shake`, `motion/spring-track`. Proposal: hand/shake + param mode=kick.

Per shot at 0.5, 1.2, 1.9 s: k goes 0 → 1 in 50 ms outCubic, then back by 1 − spring k220 d18; kicks add up. World offset x +4k px, y −9k px, rotation −0.57k° (−0.01 rad). Muzzle ring r 4 → 16 (stroke 2) over 300 ms inOut in the world; hit-marker diagonals on the crosshair for 150 ms; ammo 30 → 27.

### motion/anime-stagger-grid: EXTEND · medium · effect · ae keyframes · 3 motion(s)

- [`grid-shuffle`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=grid-shuffle) **Shuffle to Grid** (List & Grid; canvas; 980 ms; easeInOutBack; partial, medium). Closest: `card/mosaic-pack`, `text/char-slam`. Proposal: motion/anime-stagger-grid + param scatter.

Reverse to scatter: all at once in 400 ms power2.inOut.
- [`grid-spiral`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=grid-spiral) **Grid Spiral** (List & Grid; canvas; 1100 ms; easeOutCubic; partial, low). Closest: `motion/anime-stagger-grid`. Proposal: motion/anime-stagger-grid + param order.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/grid-spiral`, batch 3.7): 3×3 tiles appear in a clockwise spiral from the centre: row-major indices 4, 5, 8, 7, 6, 3, 0, 1, 2; each scales 0 → 1 in 320 ms back.out, starting 128 ms after the previous (whole run ≈1.35 s); the first tile in the accent colour.

Closest: `motion/anime-stagger-grid`, `title/wordmark-tiles`. Proposal: motion/anime-stagger-grid + param cells (cut the target picture into N x M tiles).

Tiles appear in a fixed seeded random order; tile k starts at k x 28 ms, scale 0 -> 1 about its own centre + opacity 0 -> 1 over 450 ms easeOutCubic; ~900 ms total; at the end the tiles join with no gaps (swap to the whole picture). Exit: same in a scrambled order, 200 ms each, 12 ms apart, ease-in cubic.

### motion/collect-arc: NEW · medium · effect · ae keyframes · 3 motion(s)

- [`catalog-game-hud-coin-collect`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-hud-coin-collect) **Coin Collect** (Game HUD; canvas; 3400 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `motion/anime-path`, `data/count-up`. Proposal: motion/collect-arc (new).

Each arrival: wallet squash 280 ms (1.12 / 0.88 sine), counter 100 → 125 (+5) pops 1.22 in accent, '+5' rises 18 px in 400 ms outCubic, fades 160-340 ms. Reset 2.9-3.25 s.
- [`catalog-game-reward-currency-rain`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-reward-currency-rain) **Currency Rain** (Rewards & Loot; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `effect/confetti`, `data/count-up`. Proposal: motion/collect-arc (new) mode=rain.
  Spec (verified; import as `motion/currency-rain-catch`, batch 3.5): px @320x200. 10 gems from seeded x 40-280 px fall into a bag in 550 ms each (y −12 → bag top in-quad, x out-quad to the bag centre ±5 px, fade in over the first 10 %), starting 0.2 s + i × 130 ms + up to 50 ms seeded jitter. Each catch: bag squash 10 % (scaleX 1.1 / Y 0.9, 220 ms half-sine), counter '+N×50' pops 15 %; accent once all 10 are in (+500). Loop fade 2.44-2.8 s.
- [`catalog-game-progress-collection-complete`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-progress-collection-complete) **Collection Complete** (Progression & Quests; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, low). The item flies on a quadratic curve (290,186) via (300,40) into the slot in 600 ms inOutQuad (0.4-1.0 s), one full turn, size 1.3 → 1. Lands with squash scaleX = 1 + 0.25 e^(−14t) cos(30t), scaleY = 1/scaleX; ring r 14 → 32 in 450 ms outCubic. Cells pulse 8 % (250 ms half-sine) in a wave 50 ms apart from 1.15 s, outlines accent; counter 5/6 → 6/6 at 1.0 s; label 0.8 → 1 (250 ms back.out, 1.35 s).

- [`catalog-ui-spatial-orbit-context`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-spatial-orbit-context) **Orbiting Context Menu** (Spatial UI & Depth; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, medium). Closest: `flow/menu-morph`. Proposal: ui/radial-menu (new) mode orbit.

Three items r15 end on a radius-62 orbit at −150, −90, −30°; each starts 130° back along the orbit and slides to its slot on a spring k150 d17, 80 ms apart from 0.55 s; icons stay upright; scale 0.6 → 1; a fading accent arc trail (2 px, gone by 2.0 s). Exit at 2.95 s (+50 ms each): continue 78° along the orbit and fade, 350 ms power2.in.

Ticks at 0.95, 1.3, 1.65, 2.0 s: green '−6' rises 18 px in 600 ms outCubic, fades 350-600 ms; target squash 6 % (200 ms half-sine); HP bar −12 % per tick; 4 tick dots light.

- [`tx-neon-flicker`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-neon-flicker) **Neon Flicker-On** (Colour & Light; text; 900 ms; steps; partial, medium). Closest: `text/caption-neon-glow`, `house/twinkle`. Proposal: text/glow (new) mode flicker (seeded per-char flicker-on, layered glow).

Lit: colour + glows 4 / 11 / 19 / 40 px (CSS text-shadow, whitish core #fff4fb; demo blurs 18 / 8 / 3 px). Each char starts 80 ms after the previous (from 0.35 s) and steps through one of 3 seeded level patterns, e.g. 1 → 0.15 → 1 → 0.35 → 1 at 0 / 160 / 240 / 520 / 600 ms, then stays lit (<3 flashes/s). Wall glow: radial accent 22 % × average level. Off 3.0–3.25 s; loop 3.6 s.
- [`tx-glow-pulse`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-glow-pulse) **Glow Pulse** (Colour & Light; text; 667 ms; ease-out; partial, medium · origin other:troika-three-text). Closest: `house/glow-card`, `effect/beat-accent`. Proposal: text/glow (new) mode pulse.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/glow-pulse`, batch 3.6): Repeat at 1.5 Hz (period 0.667 s): glow blur 4 → 16 px, glow opacity 0.55 → 1, outline 0 → 8 % of font size at 50 % alpha; envelope: 15 % attack (easeOutQuad), then 85 % decay as (1 − x)^2.5 (fast fall, slow tail); core slightly whiter at the peak. CSS keys 0 / 15 / 100 % ease-out. Loop 2 s (3 beats).

### text/line-by-line-slide: EXTEND · medium · effect · ae keyframes · 2 motion(s)

- [`tx-parallax-lines`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-parallax-lines) **Line Parallax** (Scroll; text; 4000 ms; linear (scroll); partial, low). Closest: `camera/scroll-story`. Proposal: text/line-by-line-slide + param parallax (per-line drift speed).

Progress p keyed 0.5 (0–0.3 s) → 1 (1.2 s, hold to 1.7 s) → 0 (2.8 s, hold to 3.3 s) → 0.5 (4.0 s), cubic-bezier(.65,0,.35,1). - [`tx-line-spacing-collapse`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-line-spacing-collapse) **Line-Spacing Collapse** (Layout & Reflow; text; 1000 ms; easeInOut; partial, medium). Closest: `text/line-by-line-slide`. Proposal: text/line-by-line-slide + param mode: collapse.

### text/marquee: NEW · medium · effect · ae keyframes · 2 motion(s)

- [`tx-scroll-velocity-marquee`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-scroll-velocity-marquee) **Scroll Velocity Marquee** (Scroll; text; 5000 ms; spring (damping 50, stiffness 400); partial, medium · origin magicui). Closest: `title/perspective-marquee`. Proposal: text/marquee (new) + param speed keys (spring).

Speed = direction × (1 + boosts) × base (≈46 / 40 px/s in a 320 px demo). Boost events at 0.6 s (+4, leftward), 2.3 s (+4, direction flips), 3.9 s (+2.5, flips back): each rises over 0.25 s power1.out then decays e^(−4t). Position = integral of speed, scaled so the 5 s loop moves whole copies (seamless).

Demo rows: 26 px filled ink moving left; 30 px in the background colour on a full-width accent band 1.64 em tall moving right; 26 px outline only (1.2 px) moving left; each moves a whole number of copies per 6 s loop (≈40 px/s in a 320 px demo). Both frame edges fade into the background over 34 px.

### text/pressure: NEW · medium · effect · ae png · 2 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: react-bits (2).

- [`tx-text-pressure`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-text-pressure) **Text Pressure** (Variable Fonts; text; 300 ms; linear; partial, medium · origin react-bits). Closest: `text/weight-wave`, `text/variable-flex`. Proposal: text/pressure (new): per-char wght / wdth from distance to a simulated cursor, line width held.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/text-pressure`, batch 3.7): A cursor sweeps left ↔ right across the line as a cosine every 4 s (amplitude half the line width + 14 px, small vertical bob). Per char f = smoothstep(1 − /dx/ / md), md = max(70 px, 0.45 × line width) (horizontal distance); wght = 100 + 800f; width factor 0.55 + 1.05f (source wdth 5–200; RFlex 25–151); then all widths are rescaled so the line keeps its rest width (far letters condense); min font size 24 px. Loop 4 s.
- [`tx-variable-proximity`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-variable-proximity) **Variable Proximity** (Variable Fonts; text; 300 ms; gaussian falloff; partial, low · origin react-bits). Closest: `text/weight-wave`. Proposal: text/pressure (new) mode proximity (weight + opsz only, positions fixed).
  Spec (verified; import as `text/variable-proximity`, batch 3.7): Simulated cursor path; per char wght 400 → 900 and opsz 9 → 40 by a Gaussian falloff of distance (radius 50–100 px); positions never move. Demo: k = exp(−2.5·d²/70²), wght = 400 + 500k (no opsz), accent tint 50 %·k; cursor glides (cubic-bezier(.4,0,.2,1)) from below the frame to 25 % across the word at 1.0 s, holds, to 75 % at 2.3 s, holds, leaves at 3.6 s; loop 4 s.

### background/speed-lines: NEW · medium · block · ae expression · 1 motion(s)

While z < 0.4 the copy is filled accent; nearer copies are outline only (1.6 px on screen, opacity × 1.2). Draw far to near. Seamless loop (4.2 s demo).

Then colour toggles accent ↔ light every 0.125 s for 0.75 s (4 Hz × 3), text colour inverted. 6 frames after the wipe the headline (28 px heavy) slides x 140 px → 0 with skewX 17° → 0, 0.2 s expo.out, clipped to the banner (no fade); then static. ### text/noise-dissolve: NEW · medium · effect · ae png · 1 motion(s)

- [`tx-noise-dissolve-out`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-noise-dissolve-out) **Noise Dissolve** (Exit; text; 1200 ms; linear; missing, medium). Closest: none. Proposal: text/noise-dissolve (new).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/noise-dissolve`, batch 3.7): Fixed noise mask on the text: fractalNoise baseFrequency 0.8, 2 octaves, seed 3 (canvas demo: 0.6 × value noise at 0.09/px + 0.4 per-cell hash, 2 px cells). A threshold rises linearly over 1.2 s (demo 1.0–2.2 s, −0.06 → 1.04; recipe brightness +100 → −100) and every cell whose noise is below it is cut out; cells within 0.07 above the threshold are painted accent as a thin burning rim. Loop: text fades back 2.6–3.1 s; loop 3.2 s.

### text/paper-fold: NEW · medium · effect · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`tx-paper-fold`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-paper-fold) **Paper Fold** (3D & Depth; text; 700 ms; power2.out; missing, medium). Closest: none. Proposal: text/paper-fold (new).

Panel k unfolds ±81° (alternating) → 0 in 0.7 s power1.out from 0.35 + 0.12 k s. Shade: background colour over each panel at /sin angle/ × 0.25 (even) / 0.75 (odd). Exit: refold from the last panel, 2.65 s + 0.08 × (3 − k), 0.45 s power1.inOut. Perspective ≈2.3 × card height (recipe 800 px). Loop 3.6 s.

### title/perspective-marquee: EXTEND · medium · block · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`scroll-marquee`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=scroll-marquee) **Scroll Marquee** (Web Scroll; canvas; 1250 ms; linear; partial, medium). Copies tile the band and move left by one unit width (sum of word widths + gaps) every 2.5 s, linear, seamless wrap. Thin grid lines 8 px above and below the band.

### title/wordmark-tiles: EXTEND · medium · block · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`mosaic-flip`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=mosaic-flip) **Mosaic Flip** (Effects & Transitions; canvas; 1150 ms; easeInOutCubic; partial, medium). Closest: `title/wordmark-tiles`, `background/pixel-worlds`. Proposal: title/wordmark-tiles + params faceA / faceB pictures.

Tile (row r, col c) starts at (r + c) x 114 ms, rotateY 0 -> 180 deg over 450 ms easeInOutCubic about its centre; swap faces at 90 deg (never show a back face), lift scaleY 1 + 0.08 x sin(angle). Diagonal wave top-left to bottom-right, ~1.15 s. Return wave reversed, 40 ms apart, 350 ms per tile.

### transition/brush-wipe: NEW · medium · effect · ae png · 1 motion(s)

- [`tr-brush`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tr-brush) **Brush Wipe** (Transitions; dom; 600 ms; easeOutCubic; partial, medium). Closest: `hand/scribble-transition`, `hand/pencil-draw`. Proposal: transition/brush-wipe (new).

Straight polygon edges in the demo; ragged dry-brush ends are optional.

A 1 px edge line and a 3×24 px grip bar (10 px in from the edge) ride each inner edge. Closing: the same move reversed in 450 ms. (Demo frame 180×120 px, radius 10 px.)

  Spec (corrected; import as `transition/gravity-drop`, batch 3.1): Outgoing scene stays on top and falls: y 0 -> +120 % of frame height and rotation 0 -> 10 deg about its top centre, 700 ms easeInQuad; the incoming scene sits still underneath from the start. Demo: +130 %, 20 deg, ease-in cubic. HF showcase-other ships a Gravity Drop.

### transition/pixel-dissolve: NEW · medium · effect · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

  Spec (verified; import as `transition/pixel-dissolve`, batch 3.1): Frame divided into square cells (16 px on a 320 px frame = 5 % of width); each cell gets a fixed seeded threshold 0..1; progress 0 -> 1 linear over 600 ms; a cell switches from old to new scene on the frame progress passes its threshold (hard per-cell cut, no fade).

### text/variable-axis: EXTEND · low · effect · ae png · 5 motion(s)

- [`catalog-kinetic-type-variable-font-pulse`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-kinetic-type-variable-font-pulse) **Variable Font Pulse** (Variable Fonts; canvas; 4200 ms; linear; partial, low). Tracking moves the other way, 8 px → 0.8 px (0.2 → 0.02 em), so the word width stays constant (the demo fakes weight with a 0–6 px stroke on a 200 face: tracking = 8 − 1.2 × stroke).
- [`tx-width-stretch`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-width-stretch) **Variable Width Stretch** (Variable Fonts; text; 600 ms; ease-in-out; partial, low).   Spec (verified; import as `text/width-stretch`, batch 3.7): Split chars (RFlex); each char wdth 75 ↔ 125, 0.6 s sine.inOut, yoyo repeat (or one swing per beat cue), char i delayed 40 ms; height unchanged, the line width breathes (chars reflow). Demo: wdth = 100 − 25·cos(π(t − 0.04 i)/0.6), beat dots every 0.6 s; loop 2.4 s. CSS: 0.6 s ease-in-out alternate.
- [`tx-weight-breathe`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-weight-breathe) **Weight Breathe** (Variable Fonts; text; 3000 ms; ease-in-out; partial, low). Closest: `text/variable-axis`. Proposal: text/variable-axis + params yoyo / repeat, ease.
  Spec (verified; import as `text/weight-breathe`, batch 3.7): Whole word wght 100 → 900 over 3 s sine.inOut, then 900 → 100 over 3 s, repeat (yoyo); positions fixed (glyphs grow in place). Demo: wght = 100 + 800 × (1 − cos(2πt/6))/2 with a breathing ring behind; loop 6 s. CSS: 3 s ease-in-out alternate infinite.
- [`tx-slant-swing`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-slant-swing) **Slant Axis Swing** (Variable Fonts; text; 2000 ms; ease-in-out; partial, low). Closest: `text/variable-axis`, `text/weight-wave`. Proposal: text/variable-axis + params axis slnt, split chars, stagger, yoyo.
  Spec (verified; import as `text/slant-swing`, batch 3.7): Split chars; slnt 0 → −10 → 0 (RFlex range −10..0), 2 s per swing sine.inOut, loop, char i delayed 80 ms; pivot on the baseline. Demo: slnt = −10 × (1 − cos(2π(t − 0.08 i)/2))/2 drawn as a skew about the baseline; loop 2 s. Fallback without a slnt axis: skewX(slnt deg), origin 50 % 100 %.
- [`tx-width-slant-scroll`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-width-slant-scroll) **Width/Slant Velocity** (Variable Fonts; text; 800 ms; spring; partial, low). Closest: `text/variable-axis`, `motion/spring-track`. Proposal: text/variable-axis + param follow (axis value from a speed track on ctx.track springs).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/speed-width-slant`, batch 3.7): v = speed of a scroll / pan move normalised to 0..1 (demo: 216 px over 0.3–1.8 s, ease (.3,0,.7,1); GSAP /velocity/ / 2000), smoothed by a spring (demo k 200, c 16 ≈ 11 % overshoot; GSAP quickTo 0.8 s power3); wdth = 75 + 50v (condensed 75 at rest, 125 at full speed), slnt = −12v (RFlex clamps at −10), pivot on the baseline; when the move stops v springs back to 0. Loop 3.2 s.

### text/cursor-field: NEW · low · effect · ae expression · 4 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: react-bits (1), other:motion (1).

- [`tx-magnetic-letters`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-magnetic-letters) **Magnetic Letters** (Pointer; text; 400 ms; power3.out; missing, low). Closest: `cursor/magnetic`. Proposal: text/cursor-field (new), mode magnet.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/magnetic-letters`, batch 3.8): Scripted cursor (cubic-bezier .65,0,.35,1 between keys): off-frame to 0.35 s → 18 % into the text, 30 px below centre (1.1 s) → 22 % from the right end, 18 px above (2.0 s) → 8 % from the right, 14 px below (2.6 s) → off (3.35 s). Char target = (cursor − char) × 0.4 × (1 − d / 100 px) inside 100 px, else 0, followed with a 0.4 s power2.out response (last 0.6 s summed). Tint to accent with displacement (full at 16 px). Loop 4.2 s.
- [`tx-proximity-repel`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-proximity-repel) **Pointer Proximity Repel** (Pointer; text; 300 ms; lerp 0.15 (exponential); missing, low). Closest: none. Proposal: text/cursor-field (new), mode repel.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/proximity-repel`, batch 3.8): Scripted cursor: bottom-left off-frame (0.4 s) → just left of the text (0.9 s) → straight across the line, linear, to just past its end (2.3 s) → out bottom-right (2.85 s). Char target = push away from the cursor of 24 px × (1 − d / 64 px) inside 64 px (recipe R 120, max 30); it closes 15 % of the gap per 60 fps frame. Tilt 0.012 rad per px of x push; tint to accent (full at 14 px); faint ghost at rest while displaced. Loop 4 s.
- [`tx-proximity-scramble`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-proximity-scramble) **Proximity Scramble** (Pointer; text; 1200 ms; none (linear); partial, low · origin react-bits). Closest: `text/anime-scramble`, `text/scramble-reveal`. Proposal: text/cursor-field (new), mode scramble.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/proximity-scramble`, batch 3.8): Scripted cursor: bottom-left off-frame (0.4 s) → the text's left end, 22 px below centre (0.9 s) → right end (2.5 s) → out top-right (3.0 s). A char within 54 px (recipe 100) scrambles until 1.2 s × (1 − d / R) after its nearest pass (cursor sampled at 30 fps): '.' or ':' (seeded, swapped every 0.08 s) in mono 700 accent over a 55 % ghost of the real char, with an accent bar under it shrinking with the time left. Loop 4.4 s.
- [`tx-hover-word-lift`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-hover-word-lift) **Hover Word Lift** (Pointer; text; 250 ms; spring(400, 26); missing, low · origin other:motion). Closest: `ui/avatar-group-hover`. Proposal: text/cursor-field (new), mode lift.
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/hover-word-lift`, batch 3.8): Sentence 34 px, up to 3 lines. Scripted cursor visits each word centre in order, evenly from 0.9 s to 3.5 s, resting ±min(0.18, 1.3 / n) s on each, then exits by 4.0 s. The hovered word rises 0 → −7 px (recipe −6) on a spring k 400, c 26 (ζ≈0.65, settles ~0.3 s) and back when left; colour tints to accent and a 1.5 px accent underline fades in, both by lift / 7. Loop 4.6 s.

- [`lk-vignette`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=lk-vignette) **Vignette Breathe** (Texture & Look; dom; 3000 ms; easeInOutSine; partial, low). Closest: `effect/vignette`. Proposal: effect/vignette as an overlay preset + param breathe.

Spot: same outline at 2.2 px with a diagonal yellow → accent → blue gradient and a 14 px accent glow, seen through a radial mask r 62 px (≈19 % of width; 1 → 0.8 at 55 % → 0) on a scripted cursor (enters 2.0 s, crosses to 3.8 s, exits 4.3 s); spot in 1.6–2.0 s, out 3.9–4.3 s. Fade 4.45–4.9 s. Loop 5 s.

### text/rsvp: NEW · low · effect · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`tx-rsvp-flash`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-rsvp-flash) **RSVP Word Flash** (Captions & Subtitles; text; 200 ms; steps (instant); missing, low). Closest: `text/caption-kinetic-slam`. Proposal: text/rsvp (new).
  Spec (verified; import as `text/rsvp-flash`, batch 3.3): One word at a fixed spot, hard cut every 0.2 s (300 wpm), × 1.5 after punctuation or for words over 8 letters; from 0.35 s the list (≤ 10 words) repeats to fill ~2.4 s, cut at 3.85 s. ORP letter (index floor(0.35 × length)) accent, centred between two short accent ticks; the part before right-aligned to it, the part after left-aligned. Size: largest ≤ 40 px (weight 700) keeping every word within ±140 px; two thin frame lines 60 px apart. Loop 4.2 s.

### text/scramble-reveal: EXTEND · low · effect · ae expression · 1 motion(s)

- [`tx-char-code-cycle`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-char-code-cycle) **Character Offset Cycle** (Typing & Decoding; text; 1000 ms; easeOutQuad; partial, low). Closest: `text/scramble-reveal`, `text/anime-scramble`. Proposal: text/scramble-reveal + param mode offset (ordered code-point steps, not random).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/char-offset-cycle`, batch 3.4): Per char: shown char = target shifted forward k code points inside its class, wrapping (a–z, A–Z, 0–9, hiragana, katakana, CJK ideographs, fullwidth A–Z / a–z); k = ceil(10 × (1 − p_i)) (AE Character Offset 10 → 0); p_i = easeOutQuad over 1 s per char, chars staggered min(120 ms, 0.9 s/n) left → right from 0.4 s; integer steps, no fade; accent while k > 0. Demo loop: all ease back to +10 over 3.0–3.5 s (inQuad); loop 3.6 s. Mono font keeps widths.

### text/spring-chain: NEW · low · effect · ae expression · 1 motion(s)

- [`tx-spring-chain`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-spring-chain) **Spring Chain Follow** (Glyph Physics; text; 1900 ms; spring chain; missing, low). Closest: none. Proposal: text/spring-chain (new).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/spring-chain`, batch 3.3): Lead char path from 0.35 s over 1.9 s, progress e = cubic-bezier(.65,0,.35,1): offset x = 0.85 em × sin(2πe), y = −1.35 em × sin(πe) (demo 34 / 54 px at 40 px), then home. Every other char, stepped at 60 fps from the start: target = previous char's position last frame + its rest offset; v = (v + (target − x) × 0.2) × 0.6, same for y; the first char of a new line follows the lead instead. Each char rotates by its link angle vs rest, clamped ±0.7 rad. Loop 4.2 s.

### text/stripe-shadow: NEW · low · effect · ae png · 1 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: magicui (1).

- [`tx-line-shadow`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-line-shadow) **Line Shadow Text** (Colour & Light; text; 15000 ms; linear; missing, low · origin magicui). Closest: none. Proposal: text/stripe-shadow (new).
  Spec (verified; import as `text/stripe-shadow`, batch 3.6): A copy of the text offset +0.04 em x / y behind it, filled with 45° stripes (stripe 0.06 em, colour / transparent) via background-clip; stripes scroll background-position 0 0 → 100 % −100 % over 15 s linear, loop; main text static. Demo exaggerates: offset 0.09 em, pitch 0.14 em (lines 42 % of it), main text with a 2 px background-colour outline; 3 pitches per 3 s loop.

### text/tracking-in: EXTEND · low · effect · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`tx-blend-overlap`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-blend-overlap) **Blend Overlap** (Colour & Light; text; 900 ms; ease-in-out; partial, low). Closest: `text/tracking-in`, `text/caption-blend-difference`. Proposal: text/tracking-in + param blend (per-char colour cycle with mix-blend-mode screen / multiply).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/blend-overlap`, batch 3.6): Chars coloured in a repeating 3-colour cycle (demo violet #7d64ff, cyan #22bdd6, yellow #f2c84b), mix-blend-mode screen on dark (multiply on light); letter-spacing 0 → −0.3 em (chars move toward the line centre) with scale 1 → 1.12 over 0.9 s power2.inOut so neighbours overlap into mixed colours; GSAP yoyo: hold 1.3 s, return 0.9 s. Demo: in 0.4–1.3 s, back 2.6–3.3 s; loop 3.6 s.

### text/wave-columns: NEW · low · block · ae expression · 1 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: codrops (1).

- [`tx-dual-wave-columns`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-dual-wave-columns) **Dual Wave Columns** (Scroll; text; 4000 ms; none (scrub); missing, low · origin codrops). Closest: none. Proposal: text/wave-columns (new).
  Spec (corrected; import as `text/wave-columns`, batch 3.2): Two columns at 29 % / 71 % of the width: the word list and the same list reversed, pitch 32 px. Item x = column ± 20 px (≈6 % of width; recipe 10–20 vw) × sin(0.032 × dy + 1.1), dy = px from frame centre, sign mirrored per column. Nearness h = 1 − /dy/ / 32: size 16 → 26 px, opacity 0.35 → 1, accent when h > 0.5; 26 px edge fades. Lists step one item 0.3–0.8 and 1.3–1.8 s, back 2.3–2.8 and 3.3–3.8 s, cubic-bezier(.65,0,.35,1). Loop 4 s.

### transition/whip-pan-cut: EXTEND · low · effect · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`tr-streak`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tr-streak) **Light-streak Whip** (Transitions; dom; 350 ms; power3.in; partial, low). Closest: `transition/directional-wipe`, `transition/whip-pan-cut`. Proposal: transition/whip-pan-cut + param streak (light band, cut at its centre).

### ui/compass-tape: NEW · low · block · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`catalog-game-hud-compass-lock`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-hud-compass-lock) **Compass Lock** (Game HUD; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; missing, low). Closest: none. Proposal: ui/compass-tape (new).
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/compass-lock`, batch 3.7): px @320x200. Tape 220 x 40 under a fixed pointer: heading 250 → 356° in 1.1 s outCubic from 0.3 s, plus +4° by spring k520 d20 from 1.4 s (settles at 360 = N); 2 px per degree, ticks every 5° (longer every 15°), labels N / NE / E… every 45°, edges fade by 1 − (dx/112)². Lock at 1.45 s: brackets close ±42 → ±20 px (250 ms back.out), N turns accent and grows 13 → 15 px, 'LOCK' pill scales in; 3-digit readout. Back to 250° 2.35-2.75 s inOut.

### ui/hud-pips: EXTEND · low · effect · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`catalog-game-hud-ammo-reload`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-hud-ammo-reload) **Ammo Reload** (Game HUD; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `ui/hud-pips`. Proposal: ui/hud-pips + params drop, counter.
  Spec (corrected; import as `ui/ammo-reload`, batch 3.7): px @320x200. 12 outlined slots 10 x 26, 16 px apart; rounds 4-12 drop in from 34 px above, 160 ms back.out, opacity in 50 ms, 85 ms apart from 0.55 s. Counter (mono 28 + '/ 12') steps 3 → 12 100 ms after each drop starts, with a 1.2 kick (200 ms half-sine); yellow while ≤ 3. 'RELOAD' label 0.35-1.45 s. Exit from 2.15 s: the 9 new rounds rise 16 px and fade in 140 ms outCubic, 35 ms apart from the last; the first 3 stay and the count falls back to 3.

### ui/icon-morph-beat: EXTEND · low · block · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`catalog-ui-control-mute-pop`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-ui-control-mute-pop) **Mute Pop** (Selection & Controls; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `ui/icon-morph-beat`. Proposal: ui/icon-morph-beat + pair speaker-mute.
  Spec (corrected; import as `ui/speaker-mute`, batch 3.6): Press at 0.4 s: icon scale 1 → 0.92 → 1 (half sine 0.18 s). Three arcs (r12 / 22 / 32, 3.5 px) vanish outer first, 60 ms apart, each 160 ms power2.in (radius × 0.7, fade). Slash draws top-left to bottom-right 0.75-0.95 s power2.out, 4 px light stroke over a 9 px background-coloured outline, width pulse × 1.5 (0.85-1.1 s); label ON → MUTE. Press 1.85 s: slash retracts 1.95-2.1 s power2.in, waves return inner first 70 ms apart (back.out 0.25 s).

### ui/reticle-lock: NEW · low · effect · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`catalog-game-combat-hit-confirm`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=catalog-game-combat-hit-confirm) **Hit Confirm** (Combat & Hit FX; canvas; 2800 ms; linear; partial, low). Closest: `ui/camera-scan-gate`. Proposal: ui/reticle-lock (new).
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/hit-confirm-reticle`, batch 3.7): px @320x200, reticle drawn ×1.5. Hit at 0.75 s, h = spring k700 d34 (~120 ms): four corners at 45° move radius 26 → 13 while their two 9 px legs shrink to 0 and 8 px diagonal strokes (2.5 px) grow inward, forming an X; colour light ink → accent past h 0.5; dashed circle r 26.5 fades in (80 % × h). Target flashes 70 % → 0 in 150 ms and shakes ±2 px 200 ms; 'HIT' label in 80 ms. Release 1.5-1.8 s outCubic (reverse).

### ui/scroll-feed: EXTEND · low · block · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)

- [`velocity-skew`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=velocity-skew) **Scroll Velocity Skew** (Web Scroll; canvas; 900 ms; easeOutCubic; partial, low). Closest: `ui/scroll-feed`, `camera/scroll-story`. Proposal: ui/scroll-feed + param skew (cards lean with scroll speed).
  Spec (verified; import as `ui/velocity-skew-feed`, batch 3.4): Viewport 160x158; cards 128x50 r8 every 62 px. List scrolls 0 → 190 px (0.3-0.85 s power2.inOut), holds, returns 1.6-2.15 s. Each card skewY (around its centre) = clamp(speed px/s × 0.012, −9°, +9°), smoothed by a spring k520 c30 so it is back at 0° about 180 ms after the scroll stops. Only small surfaces lean.

### ui/typed-prompt: EXTEND · low · block · ae keyframes · 1 motion(s)
Origins named by the site: budoux (1).

- [`tx-ime-conversion`](https://motion-reference.takayustudio.jp/#motion=tx-ime-conversion) **IME Kana-Kanji Conversion** (Japanese; text; 90 ms; steps (per keystroke); partial, low · origin budoux). Closest: `ui/typed-prompt`. Proposal: ui/typed-prompt + param compose (IME underline states).
  Spec (corrected; import as `ui/ime-conversion`, batch 3.5): Field 280 px, text 26 px. From 0.35 s one kana per 90 ms (≤ 1.6 s) under a dotted 1 px underline. 0.3 s after the last, Space: text turns into the conversion; each segment flashes an accent block (text in background colour) 0.3 s, 80 ms apart; underlines solid 1 px, focused segment 2.5 px accent; a 3-candidate window (first row accent) fades in 0.1–0.25 s later. 1 s later Enter commits: underlines and window gone. Caret 2 px accent, 0.5 s blink. Exit 4.45–4.75 s.
